### [1] [Stability of concentration in the Paley-Wiener space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01299)
*Denis Zelent*

Main category: math.CA

TL;DR: 该论文证明了在Paley-Wiener空间中，对每个固定时间-带宽乘积，集中度缺陷可以控制到平移的第一类椭球波函数的L²距离平方以及与同中心区间的归一化对称差平方，其中函数距离的指数是最优的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究Paley-Wiener空间中集中度的稳定性，量化集中度缺陷与最优先函数、区间之间的定量关系，并寻求最优指数。

Method: 核心思想是引入由最优优化者加权的集合差异（set discrepancy）来替代对称差，证明结合了基本的Hilbert空间论证与椭球波函数的经典性质。

Result: 集中度缺陷可控制到平移第一类椭球波函数的L²距离平方，以及与同中心区间的归一化对称差平方；所得到的函数距离指数是最优的。

Conclusion: 该工作给出了Paley-Wiener空间中集中度稳定性的定量刻画，确立了函数距离的最优指数，并提供了集合差异加权这一新工具。

Abstract: We prove stability of concentration in the Paley-Wiener space for each fixed time-bandwidth product. The concentration deficit controls the squared $L^2(\mathbb R)$-distance from a translated first prolate spheroidal wave function and the squared normalized symmetric difference from an interval at the same center. The obtained exponent for the function distance is optimal. The main idea is the introduction of the set discrepancy weighted by the best optimizers in place of the symmetric difference. The proof then combines elementary Hilbert space arguments with classical properties of the prolate spheroidal wave functions.

</details>


### [2] [Strip Decoupling Inequalities for the Paraboloid in $\mathbb{R}^3$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01567)
*Jacob Glidewell*

Main category: math.CA

TL;DR: 本文证明了 $\mathbb{R}^3$ 中条带（将抛物面 $R^{-1}$-邻域按 $1\times R^{-1/2}\times R^{-1}$ 曲大帽划分）的尖锐 $\ell^2(L^{10/3})$ 解耦估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大帽或大集合解耦不等式由 Demeter 首创，与 Fourier 限制理论中的反向平方函数猜想及限制猜想密切相关。本文旨在研究条带这一特定划分下的尖锐解耦估计，推进相关理论。

Method: 将条带解耦不等式归约为一个平面关联问题，并利用两端 Furstenberg 关联估计来处理该问题。方法受 Wang--Wu 关于限制猜想工作的启发。

Result: 证明了 $\mathbb{R}^3$ 中条带的尖锐 $\ell^2(L^{10/3})$ 解耦估计。

Conclusion: 条带解耦可通过平面关联问题与两端 Furstenberg 估计有效处理，从而在 $\mathbb{R}^3$ 中达到尖锐界。

Abstract: Large-cap or large-ensemble decoupling inequalities were initially studied by Demeter. These are intimately connected to the reverse square-function conjecture and restriction conjecture in Fourier restriction theory. By strips, we mean a partition of the $R^{-1}$-neighborhood of the paraboloid into $1\times R^{-1/2}\times R^{-1}$ curved large-caps. In this article, we prove the sharp $\ell^2(L^{10/3})$ decoupling estimate for strips in $\mathbb{R}^3$. We reduce the strip decoupling inequalities to a planar incidence problem, which is dealt with via two-ends Furstenberg incidence estimates. Our method is inspired by Wang--Wu on the restriction conjecture.

</details>


### [3] [Quantitative possibilities for Baxter's theorem in nonlinear Fourier analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01711)
*Michel Alexis,Gevorg Mnatsakanyan,Kristina Oganesyan*

Main category: math.CA

TL;DR: 本文研究单位圆上正交多项式的Baxter定理的定量版本，特别是针对SU(2)值非线性Fourier变换(NLFT)的范数与Lipschitz估计，并用数论构造证明了该方向上的若干否定性结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典Baxter定理只给出线性Fourier系数与Verblunsky系数同属ℓ¹的定性等价，缺乏范数与Lipschitz形式的定量刻画；作者希望将这一等价在SU(2)值NLFT框架下定量化，并厘清Baxter方法的适用边界。

Method: 在SU(2)值非线性Fourier变换背景下，探讨带范数与Lipschitz估计的定量Baxter型定理；核心是一个数论构造，用以构造反例，辅以一个肯定性结果，并分析Baxter原有方法的局限。

Result: 通过数论构造得到NLFT的一些否定性结果，表明该类定量估计并非普遍成立；同时给出一个肯定性结果，说明在特定条件下定量定理仍可成立。

Conclusion: NLFT的定量Baxter型定理不能简单照搬定性结论，Baxter方法存在本质限制；定量化需要额外的结构条件，数论构造为刻画这些边界提供了有效工具。

Abstract: Baxter's classical theorem for orthogonal polynomials on the unit circle establishes that the linear Fourier coefficients of a measure are in $\ell^1$ if and only if the nonlinear coefficients, i.e., the so-called Verblunsky coefficients, are in $\ell^1$. However, this equivalence is purely qualitative. We explore possible formulations of such quantitative theorems with norm and Lipschitz estimates for the $SU(2)$-valued nonlinear Fourier transform, for which the analog of Baxter's theorem has been recently obtained. In particular, the highlight of this paper is a number-theoretic construction for the NLFT which proves some negative results in this direction. We also prove a positive result and discuss the limitations of Baxter's method.

</details>


### [4] [Positive Bidiagonal Factorizations: Combinatorial Structure, Spectral Theory, and Matrix Continued Fractions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26066)
*Manuel Mañas*

Main category: math.CA

TL;DR: 本文研究半无限带状矩阵的规定正双对角分解，揭示任意上下带宽下的谱、逼近与可积结构，建立循环Darboux轨道与混合型Favard理论、Christoffel变换及Toda流的联系，并给出矩阵连分式的Padé型逼近与收敛性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 半无限带状矩阵的双对角分解在谱理论、逼近论与可积系统中具有基本意义，但任意上下带宽情形下其循环置换、正分解结构与逼近性质尚缺乏系统刻画，本文旨在统一处理并建立其与Favard理论、Christoffel变换及可积流的联系。

Method: 采用规定正双对角分解，证明因子的每个循环置换都存在规范化的下-上正分解；利用Christoffel词刻画最小高度正重构及达到该高度的循环积；通过因子转移诱导矩阵Christoffel变换；借助平面网络构造正径向Stieltjes矩序列；对互素带宽构造径向矩形变给出稀疏Lax族的行列式解；保留因子顺序构造矩阵连分式，研究其Padé型接触、分母分解、后向求值与误差界。

Result: 矩阵具两个块位移，互素带宽时退化为两条对角线；有限非零谱位于星形上，平面网络产生正径向Stieltjes矩序列；紧径向情形下星支撑表示测度由原点条件与负分数矩刻画；互素带宽下径向矩形变同步循环Toda流；有界非负因子时连分式收敛子单调收敛到容许域中最小非负解，并在Favard识别下逼近混合型Weyl矩阵；无界分解保留形式逼近，解析收敛需闭合实现与稳定性假设。

Conclusion: 规定正双对角分解为连接谱理论、逼近论与可积系统提供了统一框架，Christoffel词与矩阵Christoffel变换揭示了循环Darboux轨道与混合型Favard理论的对应关系，矩阵连分式给出有效的Padé型逼近与误差控制；Piñeiro与Jacobi型系统等应用表明该框架可获得全局正系数可积解。

Abstract: Prescribed positive bidiagonal factorizations of semi-infinite banded matrices reveal spectral, approximation, and integrable structures for arbitrary lower and upper bandwidths. Every cyclic permutation of the factors admits a normalized lower--upper positive factorization. Under suitable degree conditions, factor transfers induce matrix Christoffel transformations, linking the cyclic Darboux orbit to mixed-type Favard theory.
  Christoffel words determine minimum-height positive refactorizations and the cyclic products that reach them. The associated matrices have two block displacements, reducing to two diagonals for coprime bandwidths. Their finite nonzero spectra lie on a star, and planar networks yield positive radial Stieltjes moment sequences. In the compact radial case star-supported representing measures are characterized by conditions at the origin and negative fractional moments. For coprime bandwidths, a radial moment deformation gives determinant solutions of a sparse Lax hierarchy and synchronizes the cyclic Toda flows.
  Retaining the prescribed factor order yields matrix continued fractions with explicit Padé-type contact, denominator factorizations, backward evaluation, and error bounds. For bounded nonnegative factors, the convergents are monotone and converge to the least nonnegative solution in the admissible domain. Under the Favard identification, they approximate the mixed-type Weyl matrix. Unbounded factorizations retain the formal approximation; analytic convergence requires a closed realization and stability hypotheses, with an additional identification for measure-defined Weyl matrices. Piñeiro and Jacobi-like systems give applications, including global positive coefficientwise integrable solutions under the stated positivity and AT hypotheses.

</details>

### [5] [Minimal Wittstock Envelopes of Hermitian Completely Bounded Maps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00114)
*Mohsen Kian*

Main category: math.OA

TL;DR: 研究Hermitian完全有界映射的完全正对称优控元（Wittstock包络）结构，给出最小包络存在性及最小优控元存在的判定准则，并定义包络相对模与视角。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Hermitian完全有界映射通常不具备标准的完全正绝对值的类比，需要研究其完全正对称优控元集合的结构与性质，以理解这类映射的正性逼近问题。

Method: 通过研究完全正对称优控元的有序集E(Φ)（Wittstock包络），分析其极小元结构；利用谱投影的中心支撑给出最小优控元存在的判据；在有限维情形借助Choi表示进行刻画；并基于包络相对模定义Hermitian完全有界映射的包络相对视角。

Result: 每个Wittstock包络可典范地确定其下方的一个极小包络，但极小包络未必唯一；给出了在固定包络下方最小完全正优控元存在的中心支撑判据；有限维下最小完全正优控元存在当且仅当Φ或-Φ为完全正；包络相对视角推广了Okayasu的完全正映射视角，在完全正情形退化为通常视角，且一般依赖所选Wittstock包络。

Conclusion: Herrera完全有界映射缺乏典范完全正绝对值，但可通过Wittstock包络的有序结构刻画最优完全正优控元，并由此建立包络相对模与视角理论。

Abstract: A Hermitian completely bounded map $Φ:\mathscr{A}\to B(\mathcal{H})$
  need not have a canonical completely positive analogue of an absolute value. We study this problem through the ordered set $\mathsf{E}(Φ)$
  of completely positive symmetric majorants of $Φ$, which we call Wittstock envelopes.
  We show that every Wittstock envelope canonically determines a minimal Wittstock envelope below it. However, minimal envelopes need not be unique. We therefore study the stronger question of when a least completely positive majorant exists. We obtain a criterion, in terms of central supports of spectral projections, for the existence of least majorants below a fixed envelope. In finite dimensions, the Choi representation gives a simple characterization: a least completely positive majorant exists if and only if \(Φ\) or \(-Φ\) is completely positive.
  Finally, we use the envelope-relative modulus to define envelope-relative perspectives for Hermitian completely bounded maps. These constructions extend Okayasu's completely positive map perspectives, reduce to the usual perspective in the completely positive case, and remain dependent in general on the chosen Wittstock envelopes.

</details>


### [6] [A quasidiagonal $C^*$-algebra with a nonquasidiagonal maximal tensor square](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00230)
*Mehdi Moradi*

Main category: math.OA

TL;DR: 构造了一个可分的、单位的、剩余有限维C*-代数A，其极大张量平方含真等距元；A准对角而A⊗max A不具准对角性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究C*-代数核性与准对角性在张量积下的行为，寻找极大张量平方中出现真等距元的反例，以区分A与A⊗max A的性质。

Method: 用显式可数仿射群的同余商所得有限正则表示生成A；借性质(T)子群构造极大张量平方中的对角Kazhdan投影，并用伸缩共轭使该投影等价于真子投影；通过准正则表示的两因子限制下降探测严格性，给出所需范数估计并显式构造真等距元。

Result: 得到分离、单位、剩余有限维C*-代数A，使A⊗max A含真等距元；A为拟对角，而A⊗max A非拟对角，且严格性可由准正则表示探测，真等距元被显式构造。

Conclusion: 提供了一个A与A⊗max A在拟对角性上分道扬镳的显式例子，表明极大张量积可破坏准对角性，并借Kazhdan投影与范数估计实现显式构造。

Abstract: We construct a separable unital residually finite-dimensional $C^*$-algebra $A$ whose maximal tensor square contains a proper isometry. In particular, $A$ is quasidiagonal whereas $A\tmax A$ is not. The algebra is generated by finite regular representations obtained from congruence quotients of an explicit countable affine group. A property~$(T)$ subgroup determines a diagonal Kazhdan projection in the maximal tensor square. Conjugation by a dilation makes this projection equivalent to a proper subprojection. Strictness is detected by a quasi-regular representation whose two factor restrictions descend to the specified algebra $A$. We give the norm estimate needed for this descent and construct the proper isometry explicitly.

</details>


### [7] [An induction proof of strong NF\ for amenable group $C^*$-algebras](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00231)
*Mehdi Moradi*

Main category: math.OA

TL;DR: 证明可数离散顺从群的全群$C^*$-代数是强NF的，并通过表示论构造和迹分解给出两种证明路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究可数离散顺从群的全群$C^*$-代数的结构性质，特别是其是否具有强NF性质，这涉及群$C^*$-代数分类与迹空间理论的核心问题。

Method: 利用表示论构造：借助以hyper-FC根为核、以极大ICC商为商的短正合列；研究根上的极端不变迹诱导的因子表示；对正则根迹进行Choquet分解，选取分离族使其诱导迹支撑在通常FC中心上；通过精确扭叉积商与序列UCT论证使像为拟对角；最后用忠实不可约替换提供内拟对角性所需投影。此外给出直接的迹分解变体。

Result: 证明可数离散顺从群的全群$C^*$-代数为强NF，并得到两种证明共享的步骤。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了顺从群全群$C^*$-代数的强NF性质，提供了表示论与迹分解两条互补证明路径，并明确了二者共用的关键步骤。

Abstract: We give a proof that the full group $C^*$-algebra of a countable discrete amenable group is strong NF. The representation-theoretic construction uses the exact sequence whose kernel is the hyper-FC radical and whose quotient is the maximal ICC quotient. Extreme invariant traces on the radical induce factorial representations of the group. A Choquet decomposition of the regular radical trace selects a separating family whose induced traces are supported on the ordinary FC-centre. Exact twisted crossed-product quotients and a sequential UCT argument make their images quasidiagonal. Faithful irreducible replacements then supply the projections required for inner quasidiagonality. We also give a direct trace-decomposition variant and identify the steps shared by the two proofs.

</details>


### [8] [Gromov-Hausdorff Convergence of Spectral Truncations for Noncommutative Tori](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01132)
*Vibhor Bhatt,Satyajit Guin,Palash Sharma*

Main category: math.OA

TL;DR: 该文证明非交换 n-环（n≥2）谱截断所关联算子系统的状态空间在 Connes 距离下实现 Gromov-Hausdorff 收敛，并研究截断算子系统的 C*-包络与传播数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典环的收敛结果已有（文献[20]），本文旨在将其推广到非交换 n-环的谱截断情形，刻画截断算子系统的几何极限行为及结构稳定性。

Method: 利用 n-环在非交换 n-环上的作用，并结合 Bochner 积分的适当使用，构造 C^1-近似序同构，从而建立状态空间（赋 Connes 距离）的 Gromov-Hausdorff 收敛。

Result: 证明了非交换 n-环谱截断算子系统状态空间的 Gromov-Hausdorff 收敛；经典环情形作为特例成立；并给出截断算子系统的 C*-包络与传播数，表明环的截断算子系统结构性质在形变下保持。

Conclusion: 非交换 n-环谱截断的状态空间在 Connes 距离下收敛到极限空间，经典结果为其特例，且截断算子系统的结构性（C*-包络、传播数）在形变下具有稳定性。

Abstract: We prove the Gromov-Hausdorff convergence of the state spaces, equipped with Connes' distance function, of the operator systems associated with spectral truncations for noncommutative $n$-tori, $n\ge 2$. The action of the $n$-torus on the noncommutative $n$-torus, together with an appropriate use of Bochner integrals, enables us to construct an $C^1$-approximate order isomorphism that establishes the convergence. The earlier convergence result for the classical case of tori [20] follows as a special case of our result. We conclude by studying the $C^*$-envelopes and propagation numbers of the truncated operator systems and show that the structural properties of the truncated operator systems for tori persist under deformation.

</details>


### [9] [The crystal groupoid of a compact semisimple Lie group and its flag varieties](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01208)
*Aidan Sims,Robert Yuncken*

Main category: math.OA

TL;DR: 本文引入紧半单Lie群K的晶体群胚，并研究其Steinberg代数与C*-代数的结构，证明其实现多项式函数代数及其C*-包络的晶体极限；轨道空间与Weyl群双射，拓扑由Bruhat序决定，并推广到旗流形与相关Poisson齐性空间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为将高秩子移位有限型（SFT）及Kashiwara晶体的组合框架与Lie群函数代数、C*-代数及其晶体极限联系起来，并刻画其轨道空间与Weyl群及Bruhat序的几何结构。

Method: 构造紧半单Lie群K的晶体群胚，即Kashiwara晶体逆极限上高秩SFT类比所对应的Deaconu-Renault群胚；利用Steinberg代数与C*-代数实现Matassa与第二作者意义下的晶体极限；并定义旗流形及相关Poisson齐性空间的晶体群胚，证明类似结构结论。

Result: 晶体群胚的Steinberg代数和C*-代数分别实现K的多项式函数代数及其C*-包络的晶体极限；晶体群胚的轨道空间与K的Weyl群存在双射，其拓扑由Weyl群上的Bruhat序决定；对旗流形及Poisson齐性空间建立类似结果。

Conclusion: 晶体群胚将Kashiwara晶体组合、Deaconu-Renault群胚与Lie群函数代数的晶体极限统一起来，并揭示轨道空间的Weyl群/Bruhat序结构，可推广到旗流形与Poisson齐性空间。

Abstract: We introduce the crystal groupoid of a compact semisimple Lie group $K$; it is the Deaconu-Renault groupoid of a higher-rank analogue of a subshift of finite type on an inverse limit of Kashiwara crystals. We show that its Steinberg algebra and its $C^*$-algebra realise the crystal limits of the polynomial function algebra of $K$ and its $C^*$-envelope, respectively, in the sense of Matassa and the second author. We show that the orbit space of the crystal groupoid is in bijection with the Weyl group of $K$, with topology determined by the Bruhat order on the Weyl group. We also define a crystal groupoid for flag manifolds and related Poisson homogeneous spaces, and prove the analogous structural results.

</details>

*Yuhei Suzuki*

Main category: math.OA

TL;DR: 证明自由群可在单位单AF-代数上作用，且该作用为顺从作用，从而解决了非顺从群在可分类单C*-代数上顺从作用存在性问题的自由群情形，证明依赖Baire纲定理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非顺从群在可分类单C*-代数上的顺从作用存在性问题中，自由群是最后未解决的情形，本文旨在补全这一空白。

Method: 借助Baire纲定理构造自由群在单位单AF-代数上的顺从作用。

Result: 证明了自由群存在作用在单位单AF-代数上的顺从作用。

Conclusion: 自由群情形得到解决，从而完成该存在性问题在自由群上的最后一块拼图。

Abstract: We show that free groups admit amenable actions on unital simple AF-algebras. This settles, for free groups, the last remaining case of the existence problem for amenable actions of non-amenable groups on classifiable simple C*-algebras. The proof relies on the Baire category theorem. This result was obtained with substantial assistance from GPT-6 Astra.

</details>


### [11] [An Explicit Polynomial Counterexample to Connes' Embedding Conjecture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01536)
*Jiaqi Wang,Lihong Zhi*

Main category: math.OA

TL;DR: 该论文构造了一个显式的12次Hermitian整数系数多项式f（含65个自伴变量），其在任意维数、任意自伴矩阵压缩元组上的归一化迹至少为3/4，而在某个群von Neumann代数的自伴酉元组上取值为-1，从而给出Connes嵌入猜想代数形式的显式反例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Connes嵌入猜想的代数形式等价于某个二次型模（contraction quadratic module modulo commutators）的刻画。此前反例多为存在性论证，缺少显式构造。本文旨在给出一个完全显式的、可用于代数形式反例的多项式，使反例具体化、可验证。

Method: 综合三种技术：Kun–Thom的群构造、Thom的归一化论证，以及Alekseev–Liu–Thom的谱修正定理；据此确定一个显式正整数μ，构造f=1-(PQ)^2+μ∑_{ν=1}^{825}E_ν^*E_ν+μ∑_{j=1}^{65}(1-X_j^2)^2，其中P,Q编码共轭对合，E_ν编码关系缺陷。

Result: 存在显式Hermitian多项式f（65个自伴变量、次数12、整数系数），对所有自伴矩阵压缩元组的归一化迹≥3/4，并在某群von Neumann代数的自伴酉元组上等于-1；由此对任意0≤ε<1，f+ε不属于模去交换子的压缩二次型模，构成Connes嵌入猜想代数形式的显式反例。

Conclusion: 给出了Connes嵌入猜想代数形式的显式反例多项式，将以往非构造性反例精细化、具体化，并明确给出μ及各项结构，为相关算子代数与量子信息问题的显式验证提供了工具。

Abstract: We construct an explicit Hermitian polynomial $ f $ with integer coefficients, of degree $ 12 $ in $ 65 $ selfadjoint variables, whose normalized trace is at least $3/4$ on every tuple of selfadjoint matrix contractions, in every dimension, and equals $-1$ at a specified tuple of selfadjoint unitaries in a group von Neumann algebra. Consequently, $f+\varepsilon$ lies outside the contraction quadratic module modulo commutators for $0\le\varepsilon<1$, giving an explicit counterexample to the algebraic formulation of Connes' embedding conjecture. Combining the group construction of Kun and Thom with the normalization argument of Thom and the spectral correction theorem of Alekseev, Liu, and Thom, we determine an explicit positive integer $μ$ for which $f=1-(P Q)^2+μ\sum_{ν=1}^{825}E_ν^*E_ν+μ\sum_{j=1}^{65}(1-X_j^2)^2$. Here $P,Q$ encode conjugate involutions, and the $E_ν$ encode relation defects.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Rørdam的例子是C*-代数理论中的重要反例，本文旨在以群胚语言重新刻画它，从而给出该现象的一个具体群胚模型。

Method: 选取Rørdam归纳极限构造的适当模型，取交叉积，证明其极限中产生Cartan子代数，从而构造出局部紧、第二可数、Hausdorff、étale、顺从、极小且拓扑主群的群胚，其单位空间紧。

Abstract: We construct a groupoid model for Rordam's example of a simple, nuclear, separable $C^*$-algebra containing a finite and an infinite projection. This groupoid model arises from a (crossed product of a) carefully chosen model for the inductive limit construction in Rordam's original approach, which we show to yield a Cartan subalgebra of the limit. In particular, this procedure yields a locally compact, second countable, Hausdorff, étale, amenable, minimal and topologically principal groupoid whose unit space is compact, and whose $C^*$-algebra contains an infinite and a non-zero finite projection. Hence, this groupoid is amenable, and yet does not have comparison.

</details>

*Adam Dor-On,Travis Russell*

<details>

Conclusion: 图算子系统的C*-核性被2-弦性完全刻画，弦性并非必要条件；该分类统一并拓展了此前的零散结果，并提供了区分核性与非核性的具体反例。

Abstract: We classify the set of finite simple undirected graphs whose corresponding graph operator system is C*-nuclear. We prove that the operator system associated to the graph is C*-nuclear if and only if the graph is 2-chordal, as defined by McCullough. In particular, we present the first known example of a non-chordal graph whose graph operator system is C*-nuclear, as well as the first known examples of graph operator systems which are not C*-nuclear.

</details>

### [14] [A Counterexample to Wormald's Conjecture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00086)
*James Alexander Schreib*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文给出 Wormald 猜想的一个反例：一个 16 阶三次图（$K_{3,3}$ 与一个由两份细分 $K_4$ 构造的 10 阶桥图的非交并）无法将其边划分为两个同构的生成线性森林，障碍来自奇偶性。通过附加 $K_4$ 副本，可得到所有阶数 $16+4t$ 的反例。该反例不连通，连通情形仍未解决。整个反例族已在 Lean 中无自定义公理地验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Wormald 猜想涉及图的边能否划分为两个同构的生成线性森林（即同构的生成线性森林分解）。该猜想若成立，将对图的分解理论具有重要意义，因此寻找反例有助于厘清猜想的适用范围与边界。

Method: 构造一个 16 阶三次图 $\mathrm{disjoint\ union}(K_{3,3}, G_{10})$，其中 $G_{10}$ 是由两份细分 $K_4$ 构造的 10 阶桥图。通过分析边的二染色划分：任何满足条件的划分会强制每个单色连通分支的阶数为偶数，但桥结构要求将某个 5 阶的一侧划分为这样的分支，从而产生奇偶性矛盾。随后通过附加 $K_4$ 副本推广到阶数 $16+4t$。并使用 Lean 证明助手（无自定义公理）对整个反例族进行形式化验证。

Result: 该 16 阶三次图不存在将边划分为两个同构生成线性森林的方案，构成 Wormald 猜想的反例。附加 $K_4$ 后可得无穷族反例，覆盖所有阶数 $16+4t$。反例是不连通的。形式化验证在 Lean 中完成，未使用自定义公理。

Conclusion: Wormald 猜想被证伪，但反例不连通，连通三次图的情形仍未解决。结果兼具体例构造与 Lean 形式化验证的严谨性。

Abstract: We give a counterexample to Wormald's conjecture. A 16-vertex cubic graph, the disjoint union of $K_{3,3}$ with a 10-vertex bridged graph built from two subdivided copies of $K_4$, has no partition of its edges into two isomorphic spanning linear forests. The obstruction is parity: any such partition would force every monochromatic component to have even order while requiring a five-vertex side of a bridge to be partitioned into such components. Adjoining copies of $K_4$ gives counterexamples in every order $16+4t$. The counterexample is disconnected and leaves the connected case unresolved. The full family is verified in Lean without custom axioms.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 验证Borowiecki与Broere提出的关于平面图广义全染色的猜想，即是否可用四色同时满足顶点正常染色与边色类为森林的边染色约束。

Method: 结合两种工具：(1) 对划分为四个独立集的平面图，通过补全为三角剖分得到分量计数不等式；(2) 将拟阵划分定理应用于四个图拟阵。此外给出基于六个命名背景假设的Lean 4形式化。

Result: 证明该猜想成立，且更强的结论成立：任意正常4-顶点染色都能扩展为满足条件的边染色，即边色类均为森林且边不取端点颜色。

Conclusion: 平面图广义全染色猜想获证，方法为分量计数不等式与拟阵划分定理的结合，并提供了Lean 4形式化。

Abstract: We prove the conjecture of Borowiecki and Broere that every finite simple planar graph admits a generalized total coloring with four colors: a proper vertex coloring with four colors together with an edge coloring with four colors in which each edge color class is a forest and no edge receives the color of either endpoint. In fact every proper four-coloring of such a graph extends to an edge coloring of the required kind. The proof combines a component-count inequality for planar graphs whose vertices are partitioned into four independent sets, obtained by completing to a triangulation, with the matroid partition theorem applied to four graphic matroids. A Lean 4 formalization relative to six named background assumptions is described below.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Meyerovitch–Sanadhya–Solomon 的周期存在性定理需要额外假设性质 $(\star)$，限制了其适用范围；作者希望去除这一假设，并将 $d=2$ 的 Bhattacharya 定理推广到更高维，同时给出更一般的周期存在性判据与可判定性结果。

Method: 推广 Greenfeld–Tao 的布尔正规形方法：在平移轨道闭包中构造一个构型，使其在有限指数格子的陪集上的限制要么关于秩 $d-1$ 子群不变，要么由三项仿射分数部分公式给出且密度为 $1/2$；瓦片方程将这些限制配对，诱导出一个有限二部图；通过二染色把相关限制替换为常数并保持所有瓦片方程成立。由此得到独立伴随瓦片的周期可铺性刻画，以及对含独立 $(d-1)$-子族的族类的判定算法（维数作为输入的一部分）。

Result: 证明了：对每个 $d\geq2$，$d-1$ 个独立有限瓦片若存在联合余瓦片，则存在完全周期联合余瓦片；更一般地，对任意有限族，若其联合余瓦片指示函数可表示为完全周期实函数加上有限多个有界实函数（每个在某个秩 $d-1$ 子群下不变），则存在周期解；并给出由独立伴随瓦片刻画的周期可铺性及相应判定算法。

Conclusion: 该工作移除了周期存在性定理中的性质 $(\star)$，统一并推广了 Bhattacharya 关于 $d=2$ 的结果，建立了基于独立伴随瓦片的周期可铺性刻画，并为含独立 $(d-1)$-子族的瓦片族提供了判定算法。

Abstract: For every $d\geq2$, we prove that $d-1$ independent finite tiles in $\Z^d$ admit a fully periodic joint co-tile whenever they admit a joint co-tile. Here independence means that the tiles contain the origin and every choice of one nonzero vector from each tile is linearly independent. This removes property $(\star)$ from the periodic existence theorem of Meyerovitch, Sanadhya and Solomon and recovers Bhattacharya's theorem when $d=2$. More generally, we prove periodic existence for any finite family admitting a joint co-tile whose indicator is a fully periodic real function plus finitely many bounded real functions, each invariant under a subgroup of rank $d-1$. Extending the Boolean normal-form method of Greenfeld and Tao, we obtain a configuration in the translation orbit closure whose restrictions to cosets of a finite-index lattice are either invariant under a rank-$(d-1)$ subgroup or given by a three-term affine fractional-part formula with density one half. The tiling equations pair the restrictions given by this formula and determine a finite bipartite graph. A two-coloring replaces those restrictions by constants while preserving every tiling equation. We deduce a characterization of periodic tilability by independent companions and a decision algorithm for families containing an independent $(d-1)$-subfamily, with the dimension as part of the input.

</details>

*Pingping Shao,Chengye Zhao*

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 意大利支配多项式是支配多项式与罗马支配多项式的自然类比，但其系数在固定图类上的计算此前缺乏系统研究。本文旨在填补这一空白，并揭示该计数问题本质上是受限整数组合问题。

Method: 引入亏量变量 y(v)=2−f(v)，把系数 d_I(G,2n−j) 转化为将 j 分拆成 n 个不超过 2 的部分、且满足局部邻接容量约束的组合计数；据此推导 C_n 的闭式表达式（前三项等以三项式系数减去显式枚举的禁用构型表示）。算法上采用动态规划：路径 O(n^2) 时间 O(n) 空间；一般树用后序合并带亏量参数，O(n^2)/O(n)；圈用边界条件化，O(n^2)/O(n)，并给出形式化正确性证明与复杂度分析。

Result: 获得 d_I(C_n,2n−j)（j=0,…,5）的闭式结果；给出路径、树、圈上计算 D_I 全部系数的 O(n^2) 时间、O(n) 空间动态规划算法；所有数值结果经暴力枚举验证，并附完整数据表。γ_I(P_n)=⌊n/2⌋+1 与 γ_I(C_n)=⌈n/2⌉ 引用已有文献而非重新证明。

Conclusion: 将意大利支配多项式计数统一刻画为受限整数组合问题，既给出若干闭式系数，又提供覆盖路径、树、圈的多项式时间动态规划框架，为支配型多项式系数的组合与算法研究提供了可推广的思路。

Abstract: We study the problem of counting \emph{Italian dominating functions} (IDFs) of each possible weight on paths, trees, and cycles. An Italian dominating function on a graph $G=(V,E)$ is a function $f\colon V\to\{0,1,2\}$ such that every vertex $v$ with $f(v)=0$ satisfies $\sum_{u\in N(v)}f(u)\ge 2$; the \emph{Italian domination polynomial} $D_I(G,x)=\sum_k d_I(G,k)\,x^k$ records the number $d_I(G,k)$ of such functions of weight~$k$. This polynomial is the Italian analogue of the well-studied domination and Roman domination polynomials, and computing its coefficients on a fixed graph class is, in essence, a \emph{constrained integer composition} problem: passing to the deficit variables $y(v)=2-f(v)$, each coefficient $d_I(G,2n-j)$ counts the number of compositions of $j$ into $n$ parts, each at most~$2$, that additionally satisfy a local adjacency capacity constraint. We make this connection explicit and use it to derive closed-form expressions for $d_I(C_n,2n-j)$, $j=0,\ldots,5$, as trinomial coefficients minus explicitly enumerated forbidden configurations. On the algorithmic side, we give dynamic programming algorithms that compute \emph{all} coefficients of $D_I$ on path graphs ($O(n^2)$ time, $O(n)$ space), on general trees ($O(n^2)$ time, $O(n)$ space, via a post-order merge with a deficit parameter), and on cycle graphs ($O(n^2)$ time, $O(n)$ space, via boundary conditioning), together with formal correctness proofs and complexity analyses. The known values $γ_I(P_n)=\lfloor n/2\rfloor+1$ and $γ_I(C_n)=\lceil n/2\rceil$ are recalled with references rather than reproved. All numerical results are verified against brute-force enumeration, and complete tables are provided.

</details>

Abstract: Let $X$ be a nodal curve, and $G$ be the graph dual to $X$. Conclusion: 不可避免的简单 k-排列种类随 k 呈指数级增长，说明随 k 增大，Ramsey 型不可避免结构远比 1-排列情形（仅循环排列）丰富。

Abstract: A $k$-arrangement of pseudolines is a set of bi-infinite curves in the plane such that any two of them intersect each other in exactly $k$ points, at which they cross, and it is simple if no three curves meet at a common point. Cyclic arrangements are the only simple $1$-arrangements of pseudolines that are unavoidable, in the Ramsey spirit: for each fixed $m\ge 1$, every sufficiently large simple $1$-arrangement of pseudolines has a cyclic subarrangement of size $m$. We show that, for every $m\ge 3$, the number of unavoidable simple $k$-arrangements of pseudolines of size $m$ grows exponentially with $k$, independently of $m$. For even $k$, we prove an analogous result for $k$-arrangements of pseudocircles.

</details>

Abstract: Jin, Milojević, Tomon and Zhang established a powerful recursive estimate relating the positive eigenvalues of a graph whose least eigenvalue is small in absolute value. We establish a refinement of this result, yielding improved estimates across spectral graph theory and discrepancy theory, and for Chowla's cosine problem. Recent results of Janzer, Tomon and Yip allow us to directly transfer least eigenvalue estimates to the corresponding surplus estimates.

Abstract: We study two Riordan arrays associated with the Motzkin-prefix sequence (\href{https://oeis.org/A005773}{OEIS A005773}). <details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Cooper与Dutle已证明公平决斗游戏在双方命中概率相同且p→0时，射击序列收敛到Thue--Morse序列。本文希望探究初始扰动（任意有限前缀）是否会改变这一极限行为，以及改变后的序列结构如何。

Abstract: In the fair dueling game, two players take turns shooting at each other, with the turn order defined by the following fairness criterion: the next shot is assigned to the player who is less likely to have won the duel so far. Cooper and Dutle showed that when both players have the same probability $p$ of a successful shot, the shooting sequence converges to the Thue--Morse sequence as $p \rightarrow 0$. We extend this model by forcing the sequence to begin with an arbitrary finite prefix before reverting to this fairness criterion. We prove that this initial disruption fundamentally alters the sequence's eventual behavior: in the $p \rightarrow 0$ limit, unless the initial prefix is itself a prefix of the Thue--Morse sequence or its complement, the resulting sequence is eventually periodic. Moreover, the least period is a power of 2 that is at most $4^{n+1}$, where $n$ is the length of the prefix, and the repeating block is itself a prefix of the Thue--Morse sequence.

Abstract: Let $δ^*_{k,r}$ be the fractional $K_r^{(k)}$-decomposition threshold in minimum codegree.   <summary>Details</summary>

Abstract: We prove the real-rootedness conjecture of Lin, Ma, Ma, and Zhou (2021) for multiset Eulerian-Narayana polynomials, including simplicity and negativity of all zeros after removing the factor t. Using a forest decomposition and Lagrange inversion, we derive a differential formula and a first-order factorization of the differential operator. We use these factors to show that the nonconstant gamma-coefficients and ordered gamma-zeros are nondecreasing, with corresponding bounds given by the Narayana and Eulerian polynomials. Abstract: A pentagonal geometry PENT(k,r) is a k-uniform, r-regular partial linear space with the property that the set of points not collinear with a given point x is itself a line in the geometry. Abstract: We continue the study of $k$-limited domination in graphs, a domination variant in which each vertex of a dominating set may dominate at most $k$ vertices outside the set. This concept extends classical domination by incorporating capacity constraints on dominating vertices.

As a consequence, we characterize graphs satisfying $γ_k^L(G)=\lceil \frac{n}{k+1} \rceil$. We also refine the known upper bound for graphs with $k<δ(G)$ using the $k$-limited packing number. Under this condition, we describe graphs attaining $γ_k^L(G)=n-k$. These results extend and unify previous investigations for the case $k=1$ and provide a complete characterization of graphs attaining the extreme values of the $k$-limited domination number under the considered assumptions.

Abstract: In the $q$-color online Ramsey game, Builder and Painter play on an infinite independent set of vertices. At each step, Builder draws an edge and Painter immediately assigns it one of $q$ colors. Builder aims to force a monochromatic copy of a fixed graph $H$. We prove that, for every $q \ge 2$ and $d \ge 1$, Builder can force a monochromatic copy of any $d$-degenerate graph $H$ while drawing a graph of degeneracy at most $d$. Abstract: A dominating set $D$ of a graph $G$ is called $k$-limited if every vertex of $D$ has at most $k$ neighbors outside $D$. The minimum cardinality among all $k$-limited dominating sets of $G$ is the $k$-limited domination number, denoted by $γ_k^L(G)$.

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 整数分拆的 \(t\)-交族是组合极值理论中的自然问题，规范星（含至少 \(t\) 个 1 的分拆）被认为是自然候选最大族。此前 Borg 猜想该星在更宽的参数范围内最大，需要验证其正确范围并刻画失效边界。

Method: 将恰好 \(k\) 个正整数部分的整数分拆映射为相关问题，应用 Kupavskii 的弱 spread 定理，并结合分拆的组合结构与计数估计，分析 \(t\)-交族的规模上界。

Result: 证明当 \(n\ge Ak^3\) 时，对任意固定 \(A>24\)、充分大的 \(k\) 及所有 \(1\le t<k\)，规范 \(t\)-星是最大的 \(t\)-交族。同时构造反例：对充分大 \(k\) 与任意 \(1\le d<k\)，取 \(d/4\le t\le d\) 且 \(n=\lfloor tk^2/3\rfloor\)，存在严格大于规范星的 \(t\)-交族。

Conclusion: 规范 \(t\)-星在 \(n\) 关于 \(k\) 的三次量级时确实最大，证实了 Borg 猜想在该范围内的情形；但当 \(n\) 降至约 \(tk^2/3\) 的二次量级时猜想失效，说明最大族的结构在不同交尺度下呈现更复杂的相变。

Abstract: Two integer partitions $t$-intersect if they have at least $t$ common parts, counted with multiplicity. We study the largest $t$-intersecting families of integer partitions of $n$ into exactly $k$ positive parts. The canonical $t$-star consists of the partitions containing at least $t$ ones. Applying Kupavskii's weak-spread theorem, we prove that this star is largest whenever $n\ge Ak^3$, for every fixed $A>24$ and all sufficiently large $k$, uniformly over $1\le t<k$. We also give counterexamples to Borg's conjecture at every intersection scale: for all sufficiently large $k$ and for every $1\le d<k$, one may choose $d/4\le t\le d$ and $n=\lfloor tk^2/3\rfloor$ so that a $t$-intersecting family is strictly larger than the canonical star.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 链乘积的 Schur 正性问题与小 Schröder 数、对称函数展开密切相关；已知宽度二、三的情形，需进一步判定一般宽度下的非 Schur 正性，并寻找可显式刻画的反例系数。

Conclusion: 链乘积 $\mathbf{m}\times\mathbf{n}$ 的非 Schur 正性范围被显著扩展，并给出负系数的显式多项式表达，为 Schur 正性判定提供了新的反例族与可计算工具。

Abstract: We prove that the product of chains $\mathbf{m}\times\mathbf{n}$ is not Schur positive whenever $n\ge4$ and $m\ge3n-1$. Writing $m=n+k$, we exhibit a negative coefficient indexed by $(2n+k-1,2n+k-3,\ldots,k+5,k,k,4)$ and evaluate it explicitly as $(n-2)!$ times a polynomial of degree three in $k$. Our argument refines the chain-partition method of Li, Qiu, Yang, and Zhang: rank capacity forces $n-2$ long chains, and the remaining three chains are counted by separating a middle interval with fixed row coordinates from two bounded boundary regions. Together with their theorem and the known cases of widths two and three, this shows that these products are not Schur positive for $n\ge3$, $m\ge n+5$, and for $n=2$, $m\ge8$.

</details>

Abstract: We study three recurrent double sequences whose colored diagrams encode mirrored versions of the finite-ordinal construction. Two sequences use three letters and realize variants with two and three initial symbols. A four-letter sequence realizes the von Neumann construction from one initial symbol. For each sequence we describe its cells explicitly and identify the ordered nesting of its rectangles. For four letters, we distinguish ordinary and virtual boxes and prove their assembly rules. The proofs proceed by induction along antidiagonals. For the constructions with one and three initial symbols, we reduce the recurrence to finite sets of local configurations and verify those sets by computer.   <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 作者采用加性组合与拟阵（matroid）方法。核心是把Ruzsa的原始论证与“子集和几乎不重合”（dissociated/一般位置）的结构条件结合，构造性地选出满足2^{2k}-1个不同子集和的元素组。证明可推广到奇数阶有限Abel群，并给出超饱和下界Ω(|A|^{2k}/N)，对偶数阶群给出较弱版本。随后将同一方法迁移到向量空间：把图论中偶圈极值问题翻译成F_q-可表示拟阵中无2k-圈（circuit）问题，利用秩-大小关系导出上界C q^{n/k}。对原图情形，给出Bondy-Simonovits定理及其超饱和版本的新证明。

Abstract: In 1993, Ruzsa showed that for every $k \geq 2$, there exists a constant $C$ such that every subset $A \subseteq [N]$ of size at least $C N^{1/k}$ contains $2k$ distinct elements $a_1, \ldots, a_k, b_1, \ldots, b_k \in A$ such that $a_1 + \cdots + a_k = b_1 + \cdots + b_k$. We strengthen this result by proving that the elements $a_1, \ldots, a_k, b_1, \ldots, b_k$ can be chosen to have the additional property that $\{a_1, \ldots, a_k, b_1, \ldots, b_k\}$ has $2^{2k}-1$ distinct subset sums, with the only coincidence being that $\{a_1, \ldots, a_k\}$ and $\{b_1, \ldots, b_k\}$ have the same sum.

When $q=2$, this is best possible up to the constant $C$ for all $k \geq 2$. Our methods also apply to the original graph setting and give a new proof of the Bondy-Simonovits Theorem and its supersaturation version.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Result: 证明了两条括号方式（(P⊕Q)⊕R与P⊕(Q⊕R)）确定同一个二次多项式q=h/c（当c≠0时），其图像包含P、Q、R与−T，因而归约为同一终点，从而结合律成立。该恒等式在所有特征下均有效。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了一个特征无关的、基于多项式恒等式与插值的结合律证明，统一了不同特征的论证，并将短Weierstrass情形下的插值二次多项式与Zwegers的构造联系起来。

Abstract: Let $K$ be a field and let $E/K$ be a nonsingular projective curve in general Weierstrass form. We prove associativity of its chord--tangent operation by combining polynomial factorization, formal differentiation, and quadratic interpolation. The main identity eliminates the two nonvertical lines $Y=\ell(X)$ and $Y=m(X)$ occurring in the successive additions $S=P\oplus Q$ and $T=S\oplus R$. There are $c\in K$ and a monic quadratic polynomial $h\in K[X]$ such that \[
  \ell+m+\Apol=c(X-x_S),
  \qquad
  \Gpol+\ell m=(X-x_S)h, \] and \[
  (Y-\ell)(Y-m)
  =(X-x_S)(h-cY)
  +Y^2+\Apol(X)Y-\Gpol(X). \] When $c\ne0$, the graph of $q=h/c$ contains $P,Q,R$, and $-T$. The two bracketings determine the same quadratic by ordinary or Hermite interpolation and therefore yield the same final point. Result: L(C_P,t,T) 与 t 无关，分解为每个轨道一个因子 1-wt(ν)T^{per(ν)}；分析秩等于权重为 1 的轨道上 p^{v_p(per(ν))} 之和。当 n 为 p 的幂时等于 (q/p)^n (p-1)^{n-1}，无界。对每个 q≥3 均成立。

Conclusion: 否定了 Grishkov–Logachev 问题 1.4：Carlitz 模扭的分析秩对任意 q≥3 无界；同时回答了该综述问题 3.6.1–3.6.4 在本题族上的情形。

Abstract: Grishkov and Logachev have asked (Problem 1.4 of their recent survey) whether, for a fixed finite field F_q, the analytic ranks of the twists of the Carlitz module are bounded. We show that the directed graph of the matrix M(P_{q,n},1,k) of Grishkov-Logachev has a completely explicit set of circuits: they are pairwise disjoint, none of them meets an entry involving t, and they are in bijection with the orbits of E^n under cyclic rotation, where E is the set of l in [0,q-2] with p not dividing l+1. Consequently L(C_P, t, T) does not depend on t, it factors as a product of one term 1 - wt(nu) T^per(nu) per orbit, and the analytic rank equals the sum of p^(v_p(per(nu))) over the orbits of weight 1. When n is a power of p this equals (q/p)^n (p-1)^(n-1), which is unbounded. The argument also answers questions 3.6.1 to 3.6.4 of the survey for this family.

</details>

TL;DR: 本文改进 [0,1] 上整数 Chebyshev 常数的上下界，将区间从 1.39e-3 缩小到 4.56e-4，上界由 92 因子的整数多项式给出，下界由 947 个单元上的离散测度与 206 个不可约多项式库给出，两者均通过显式证书建立。

  <summary>Details</summary>

Result: 证明了 0.4222286000 <= t_Z([0,1]) <= 0.4226846975，将原区间宽度由 1.39e-3 缩小至 4.56e-4。上下界均由显式证书建立，且可用所附脚本独立重新验证。

Conclusion: 该工作显著缩小了 [0,1] 上整数 Chebyshev 常数的已知区间，提供了可机器验证的显式证书与可复现脚本，为后续进一步精确确定该常数奠定了基础。

Abstract: The integer Chebyshev constant of [0,1] has been bracketed by 0.4213 <= t_Z([0,1]) <= 0.422685 since the work of Pritsker (2005) and of Flammang (2014). We prove
  0.4222286000 <= t_Z([0,1]) <= 0.4226846975,
  narrowing the interval from 1.39e-3 to 4.56e-4. Both bounds are established by explicit certificates. The upper bound is carried by an integer polynomial with 92 factors and explicit integer exponents; the lower bound by a discrete measure on 947 cells together with a library of 206 irreducible polynomials, through an inequality in which the terms paying for divisibility are made explicit. Two self-contained scripts, included as ancillary files, re-derive both bounds from the certificates alone.

</details>

*Thang Pham,Chun-Yen Shen,Dung The Tran,Boqing Xue*

Main category: math.NT

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究有限域上二次型诱导的"距离集"（差值的 $Q$ 值集合）的基数下界，是有限域加性组合与和积现象的自然问题；已有结果多针对特定维数或特定二次型，缺乏对一般非退化二次型、任意维数 $2m$ 的统一且最优（至对数因子）的定量估计。作者希望建立与 $p$、$m$、$Q$ 无关的绝对常数下的普适下界。

Method: 设 $p$ 为奇素数，$m\geq1$，$Q$ 为 $\mathbb{F}_p^{2m}$ 上 Witt 指数 $m-1$ 的非退化二次型。对非空集合 $E$，定义距离集 $\Delta_Q(E)=\{Q(x-y):x,y\in E\}$。作者通过加性组合工具（如特征和估计、Fourier 分析/指数和、以及二次型在高维下的结构性质）对 $|E|\geq p^m$ 的情形建立一般下界；对于平面情形 $m=1$ 采用更精细的估计，覆盖 $1\leq|E|\leq p$ 的整个范围。

Result: 当 $|E|\geq p^m$ 时，证明 $|\Delta_Q(E)|\gg \frac{p}{\log(2+p^{m+1}/|E|)}$，其中隐含常数绝对，且与 $p$、$m$、$Q$ 无关。在平面情形 $m=1$ 下，进一步证明对 $1\leq|E|\leq p$ 有 $|\Delta_Q(E)|\gg \frac{|E|}{\log(2|E|)}$，该结果在对数因子意义下达到最优。

Conclusion: 该工作给出了有限域上非退化二次型距离集的一般性下界估计，统一并推广了先前结果；平面情形 $m=1$ 的结果在对数因子内最优，表明所获估计在阶的意义上已接近极限。

Abstract: Let $p$ be an odd prime, let $m\geq1$ be an integer, and let $Q$ be a nondegenerate quadratic form on $\mathbb{F}_p^{2m}$ with Witt index $m-1$. For a nonempty set $E\subseteq\mathbb{F}_p^{2m}$, write $Δ_Q(E)=\{Q(x-y):x,y\in E\}$. We prove that, whenever $|E|\geq p^m$, $$ |Δ_Q(E)|\gg \frac{p}{\log\bigl(2+p^{m+1}/|E|\bigr)}, $$ with an absolute implied constant independent of $p$, $m$ and $Q$. In the planar case $m=1$, we also prove that $$ |Δ_Q(E)|\gg\frac{|E|}{\log(2|E|)}, \qquad (1\leq |E|\leq p), $$ which is optimal up to a logarithmic factor.

</details>

Abstract: A longstanding open problem concerning Ramanujan-Sato series is given by determining an expression for the coefficients $c_{11}(n)$ associated with Ramanujan-Sato series of level 11 with a fixed, nested, finite binomial sum for all nonnegative integers $n$, by direct analogy with the known formulas for the coefficients for lower-level Ramanujan-Sato series (and this is clarified and made explicit in our paper). This open problem was given by Cooper et al. in 2015 [J. Math. Anal. Appl.], in Cooper's 2017 textbook on Ramanujan's theta functions, and in Cooper's 2025 contribution to the Contemp. Math. series. This problem seems to have remained open. We succeed in solving this problem, by introducing and proving a double binomial sum formula for $c_{11}(n)$ that is directly analogous to the coefficient formulas for lower-level Ramanujan-Sato series.

</details>


### [53] [Perfect squares in reciprocal-sum sequences and primes that are inert in quadratic fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00765)
*Mateo Matijasevick,Santiago Rodríguez,Gregorio Salazar*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 研究了递推 x_{n+1}=k/(x_0+...+x_n) 的既约分数 a_n/b_n，刻画了使 c·a_n+b_n 恒为完全幂的 k，并给出不变量 b_n^2-(4/k)a_n^2 为有理平方/整数平方的条件及两类推广。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者关心递推序列 x_{n+1}=k/(x_0+...+x_n) 中既约分数的分子分母组合何时恒为完全幂（尤其平方），并寻找判定 k 的算术条件以及统一的不变量结构。

Method: 关键观察：令 t_n=(x_0+...+x_n)/h，则 t_{n+1}=t_n+1/t_n，且该递推下既约分数不会发生约分，由此得到 c·a_n+b_n 的精确公式并分析各素因子的影响；进一步用 Q(√-k) 中的惯性条件处理一般 k，并推广到 x_{n+1}=h^2/(x_0+...+x_n+nμh)，对应二次域 Q(√(μ^2-4))。

Result: 存在有理常数 c>0 使对所有此类序列及所有 n≥2，c·a_n+b_n 为完全幂，当且仅当 k=h^2 且 h 的每个素因子≡3 (mod 4)，此时 c=2/h 且幂为平方；一般 k 时 b_n^2-(4/k)a_n^2 恒为有理平方，且为整数平方当且仅当 k 的平方部分的素因子在 Q(√-k) 中满足惯性条件；对该递推可推广到二次域 Q(√(μ^2-4))，涵盖 Q(√-3) 与所有实二次域。此外沿任一序列，h 的每个素因子至多破坏一项。

Conclusion: 该工作通过 t_n 递推与不变量 b_n^2-(4/k)a_n^2 精确刻画了 a_n,b_n 的完全幂性质，将问题提升为二次域中的惯性条件，并实现了从高斯整数到更一般二次域的推广。

Abstract: Let $k$ be a positive integer, and consider the sequences of positive rationals with $x_0\in\N$ and $x_{n+1}=k/(x_0+x_1+\dots+x_n)$. Write $x_n=a_n/b_n$ in lowest terms. We show that there is a rational constant $c>0$ such that $c\,a_n+b_n$ is a perfect power for every such sequence and every $n\ge2$ if and only if $k=h^2$ and every prime factor of $h$ is congruent to $3$ modulo $4$; in that case $c=2/h$ and the powers are squares. The proof rests on the observation that $t_n=(x_0+\dots+x_n)/h$ satisfies $t_{n+1}=t_n+1/t_n$, a recursion under which reduced fractions never cancel. This yields an exact formula for $c\,a_n+b_n$, shows that along any single sequence each prime factor of $h$ spoils at most one term, and leads to two generalizations. For arbitrary $k$ the invariant $b_n^2-\frac4k a_n^2$ is always a rational square, and it is always an integer square exactly when the primes dividing the square part of $k$ satisfy an inertness condition in $\Q(\sqrt{-k})$. For the recursions $x_{n+1}=h^2/(x_0+\dots+x_n+nμh)$ the role of the Gaussian integers is played by the quadratic fields $\Q(\sqrt{μ^2-4})$, which include $\Q(\sqrt{-3})$ and every real quadratic field.

</details>


### [54] [Congruence Jumping in Polynomial Divisibility Systems: Arithmetic Structure and Solution Sets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00808)
*Max A. Alekseyev,Dmitry I. Khomovsky*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 研究整数系数多项式间的非零整数解系统 y|A(x), x|B(y)，提出"同余跳跃"（congruence jumping）框架，建立互反多项式跳跃规则、积分归一化判据、伴随曲面恒等式与二次情形的定量分类。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 原始问题为多项式对 (A,B) 上的整除型丢番图系统 y|A(x), x|B(y)，通常难以直接求解；作者希望通过允许多项式对发生"状态变换"把复杂系统转化为更易处理的形式，从而在尽量弱的假设下（不要求互素、首一或单位）统一处理一般与二次情形。

Method: 提出 congruence jumping：利用商变换改变多项式对，导出控制跳跃的互反多项式规则，并给出积分归一化的精确判据；构造伴随曲面恒等式以生成无限整数商链；对二次情形给出有限链的分母定量界、单侧无限链的例外界定，并将其归约为固定圆锥曲线上的 Vieta 动力学；还给出独立的 relation-lattice 判据。

Result: （1）在不假设互素/首一/单位下建立一般互反多项式跳跃规则及积分归一化判据；（2）由伴随曲面恒等式构造出无限整数商链，含显式混合次数例子；（3）给出二次有限链的定量分母界，分类非整数圆锥参数的非平凡单侧无限链；（4）构造出具有无穷多正整数点的真非单位四循环，并证明其动力学存在一致的 Pell 型线性化；（5）用 relation-lattice 判据把部分解集化为有限除子搜索。

Conclusion: 该工作以 congruence jumping 为核心工具，将多项式整除系统的求解转化为可迭代的变换与归一化问题，并对二次情形给出较完整的定量与结构刻画；同时通过非单位四循环与 Pell 线性化表明单位假设可被实质性放松。

Abstract: Let $A$ and $B$ be polynomials with integer coefficients. We study nonzero integer solutions of $$y\mid A(x),\qquad x\mid B(y),$$ using quotient transformations that may change the polynomial pair. We call this process \emph{congruence jumping}. We establish a general reciprocal-polynomial rule governing such jumps, without coprimality, monicity, or unit assumptions, and give exact criteria for when the resulting polynomial states can be normalized integrally. Companion-surface identities provide a mechanism for constructing infinite integral quotient chains, including an explicit mixed-degree example.
  The quadratic case is considerably more rigid. We obtain a quantitative denominator bound for finite chains, classify the exceptional nonconstant one-sided infinite chains with nonintegral conic parameter, and show that changing quadratic states reduce to ordinary Vieta dynamics on a fixed conic. We further construct a genuinely nonunit recurrent four-cycle with infinitely many positive integral points and show that its dynamics admits a uniform Pell-type linearization. A motivating nonunit divisibility system is analyzed through Pell orbits and changing-state ladders. Finally, an independent relation-lattice criterion reduces certain solution sets to a finite divisor search.

</details>

Abstract: Adapting the circle method developed by Frantzikinakis--Klurman--Moreira, we establish a sufficient criterion for a set of natural numbers to be multiplicatively recurrent. We apply this criterion to Piatetski--Shapiro sets with small exponents and to regular irrational nil-Bohr sets. We also study the value distribution of unimodular completely multiplicative functions along these sets.

</details>

Abstract: We construct indefinite theta representations for a family of signed generalized Nahm sums of arbitrary rank \(r\geq2\). After explicit normalization and boundary correction, one signed branch gives the even components of a \((2r-1)\)-component holomorphic theta vector associated with a lattice of signature \((1,1)\). Euler-type multiple sums provide the odd components, yielding formulas uniform in both the rank and the component index. Using Zwegers' theory, we determine the weight-one modular transformation laws of the real-analytic completion, with an explicit finite sine matrix for the \(S\)-transformation. We decompose the nonholomorphic correction into a finite sum of products of unary theta functions and one-dimensional error-function series, and compute the antiholomorphic derivative of the completion. Together with the completed transformation laws, these formulas give the inhomogeneous transformation law of the holomorphic vector. We also evaluate a linear combination of two signed branches as a difference of Jacobi product quotients. This identity and further evaluations for two families of symmetrizable matrices and their duals, combined with known results, give multiple-sum representations for every component of four associated product vectors. These representations are finite combinations of generalized Nahm sums with the prescribed matrices and symmetrizers; in the dual families, signed sums and half-period translates are allowed.

### [58] [The sixth moment of the Riemann zeta function](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01035)
*Alexandre de Faveri,Mayank Pandey*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文证明临界线上 Riemann zeta 函数的新大值估计，首次改进了自 Hardy-Littlewood (1923) 以来关于 |ζ(1/2+it)|≥T^{1/8} 的测度上界，并对所有 4<k≤12 给出 k 阶矩的改进上界，特别得到 ∫_0^T |ζ(1/2+it)|^6 dt ≪_ε T^{5/4−1/60+ε}。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临界线上 zeta 函数的大值估计与矩问题是解析数论的核心问题。自 Hardy-Littlewood 1923 年给出 |ζ(1/2+it)|≥T^{1/8} 的测度上界以来，该阈值处的估计长期未获改进；同时 4<k≤12 区间的 k 阶矩上界也亟需提升，本文旨在突破这些瓶颈。

Method: 核心技术工具是建立带平方根相位的指数和及其某些扰动的新大值估计；此类指数和出现在 zeta 函数的短二阶矩展开中，作者利用该新估计推导 zeta 的大值估计与高阶矩上界。

Result: 首次改进了 |ζ(1/2+it)|≥T^{1/8} 在 t∈[T,2T] 上的测度上界（自 Hardy-Littlewood 1923 以来）；对所有 4<k≤12 给出 k 阶矩的改进上界；特别地证明 ∫_0^T |ζ(1/2+it)|^6 dt ≪_ε T^{5/4−1/60+ε}。

Conclusion: 通过平方根相位指数和的新大值估计，本文在 zeta 大值测度与 4<k≤12 阶矩问题上取得实质性进展，六阶矩上界达到指数 5/4−1/60+ε。

Abstract: We prove new large value estimates for the Riemann zeta function on the critical line. For instance, we improve the upper bound on the measure of $t\in [T, 2T]$ with $|ζ(1/2 + it)| \geq T^{1/8}$ for the first time since Hardy-Littlewood (1923). Our results imply improved upper bounds on the $k$-th moment of zeta for every $4 < k \leq 12$. We show in particular that
  \begin{equation*}
  \int_{0}^{T} |ζ(\tfrac{1}{2} + it)|^6 \,d t \ll_\varepsilon T^{\frac{5}{4} - \frac{1}{60} + \varepsilon}.
  \end{equation*}

Attainment is established before the least extremal row $T_p(m)$ is defined. For the special family $m=p^a+1$ with $a\ge2$, we determine that least row exactly: $T_p(p^a+1)=p^{3a}+1$. The proof combines Kummer's carry theorem with an explicit leading-digit witness for the universal upper bound, a multiplicative-order construction for equality, and a separate strict-minimality argument below $p^{3a}+1$ with a fallback witness for the unique worst leading-digit pattern. The selected-GCD family itself is known in the literature; the relation to earlier results and the limits of the documented literature search are stated explicitly.

Abstract: Determining whether an algebraic number field admits a power integral basis is a classical problem, but it can be difficult for fields defined by polynomial compositions and dynamical iterates. In this paper, we study the monogeneity of compositions $f(h(x))$, where $f(x)$ and $h(x)$ are monic polynomials over an arbitrary Krull valuation ring and $h(x)$ is a trinomial. We derive explicit formulas for the discriminant of the composition and use them to characterize when $f(h(x))$ generates a monogenic field. In particular, we relate the monogeneity of the composition to that of $f(x)$ and to explicit square-free conditions on the critical values of $h(x)$. We further extend the results to polynomial iteration and obtain a criterion for the monogeneity of binomial iterates, yielding new infinite families of monogenic fields. Finally, we give quantitative results and illustrate our criteria with several examples.

Abstract: For a positive integer $m$ and a real parameter $α>-1$, consider the generalized Cesàro matrix on $\ell^2(\mathbb N_0)$ with entries $m(n-j+1)_{m-1}/(n+α+1)_m$ for $j\le n$. We give a computer-assisted proof that this operator is hyponormal if and only if $α\ge0$. The main algebraic ingredient is an explicit decomposition of an auxiliary defect operator into a positive diagonal operator and at most $m$ rank-one operators. Discrete Gram polynomials determine the signs and coefficients of these terms. For nonnegative parameters, weighted estimates reduce positivity to scalar inequalities. A uniform analytic estimate covers every $m\ge32768$, while finite rational certificates cover the remaining orders and entire parameter intervals, without parameter sampling or truncating an infinite operator. The proof also gives a uniform weighted lower bound for the self-commutator. For $-1<α<0$, finite-support vectors give negative quadratic forms for every order.

*David Norrbo*

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Abstract: We prove that the Hilbert matrix operator $H:A^2_{\ln^α} \to A^2$ is bounded when $α\geq 2$ and that $H$ does not map $A^2_{\ln^α} $ into $A^2$ when $α<2$, solving an open problem posed by B. Karapetrović and M. Jevtić. The boundedness when $α=2$ is shown as a corollary to the more general statement: the Hilbert matrix $H:\ell_v^p \to \ell_w^p$ is bounded, where $v(n) = (\ln n)^p / n$ and $w(n) = 1/n$.

</details>


### [68] [Self-improvement for absolutely Cesáro bounded operators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00271)
*Loris Arnold*

Main category: math.FA

TL;DR: 本文研究了p-绝对Cesàro有界算子与q-绝对Cesàro有界性之间的定量关系，给出了最优指标范围，并在Banach空间与L^p空间上推广了相关结论，同时构造了反例说明谱条件不能控制增长。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 绝对Cesàro有界性是遍历理论中刻画算子平均收敛行为的重要概念，但不同p阶绝对Cesàro有界性之间的精确关系、以及谱性质能否推出增长估计等问题尚未完全清楚。本文旨在确定p-绝对Cesàro有界性蕴含更强q-绝对Cesàro有界性的最优指标范围，并在不同空间上拓展相关理论。

Method: 通过算子论与Banach空间几何方法：先证明p-绝对Cesàro有界（常数K>1）蕴含q-绝对Cesàro有界对1≤q<pK/(K-1)成立，并用正加权移位说明该范围最优；在s∈(1,2]型Banach空间上证明绝对Cesàro有界蕴含q<s的q-绝对Cesàro有界并给出q=s的对数估计及‖T^n‖=O(n^{1/s-ε})的多项式增长界；在L^p空间上将结论从正算子推广到个体最终正算子；最后在ℓ^1上构造反例。

Result: （1）p-绝对Cesàro有界（常数K>1）蕴含q-绝对Cesàro有界，当且仅当1≤q<pK/(K-1)，该范围对正加权移位最优；（2）在s∈(1,2]型Banach空间上，绝对Cesàro有界蕴含所有1≤q<s的q-绝对Cesàro有界，q=s时有对数估计，并有‖T^n‖=O(n^{1/s-ε})；（3）在L^p（1<p<∞）上将绝对Cesàro有界到p-绝对Cesàro有界的蕴含关系从正算子推广到个体最终正算子；（4）对每个1≤p<∞，在ℓ^1上构造出p-绝对Cesàro有界算子T，满足σ(T)⊆D∪{1}但limsup‖T^n(I-T)‖=∞，且该构造给出对一切1≤p<∞均p-绝对Cesàro有界的单一算子。

Conclusion: 本文完整刻画了p-绝对Cesàro有界性之间的最优指标关系，并在Banach空间与L^p空间上给出了更强的蕴含结论与增长估计。ℓ^1上的反例表明仅由谱条件σ(T)⊆D∪{1}不能保证‖T^n(I-T)‖有界，即使对任意阶p-绝对Cesàro有界算子亦然，说明谱性质与绝对Cesàro有界性之间存在本质差距。

Abstract: We prove that every $p$-absolutely Cesàro bounded operator with constant $K>1$ is $q$-absolutely Cesàro bounded for $1\leq q<pK/(K-1)$. This range is optimal, even for positive weighted shifts. On Banach spaces of type $s\in (1,2]$, we prove that every absolutely Cesàro bounded operator is $q$-absolutely Cesàro bounded for every $1\leq q<s$, with a logarithmic estimate at $q=s$. We also prove the polynomial growth bound $\| T^n \| =O(n^{1/s-\varepsilon})$ for some $\varepsilon>0$. On $L^p$-spaces, $1<p<\infty$, we extend the implication from absolute Cesàro boundedness to $p$-absolute Cesàro boundedness from positive to individually eventually positive operators. Abstract: We generalise Berger's dilation theorem from numerical ranges to scaled $q$-numerical ranges. Specifically, we prove that if the scaled $q$-numerical range $\frac{W_q(A)}{q}$ is contained in the closed unit disk, then $A$ admits a unitary $ρ_q$-dilation, where \[ ρ_q=\max\left\{1,\frac{2|q|}{1+\sqrt{1-|q|^2}}\right\}. \] As a corollary we show that every disk containing $Ω_q(A)$ is a complete $ρ_q$-spectral set for $A$. At $q=1$, this recovers the classical fact that any disk containing $W(A)$ is a complete $2$-spectral set for $A$.

</details>

### [71] [Riesz transform characterization of Hardy spaces associated with Dunkl--Schrödinger operators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00703)

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Dunkl-Schrödinger算子的Hardy空间理论尚不完善，需给出与Riesz变换等价的$H_L^1$范数刻画，并推广至任意根系与非负重数函数。

Method: 结合 $H_L^1$ 的原子分解、局部Hardy空间理论以及Dunkl热核的加权积分估计，在关于立方体容许覆盖的局部积分条件与半群衰减条件下证明范数等价。

Result: $\|f\|_{H_L^1}\asymp\|f\|_{L^1(dw)}+\sum_{j=1}^N\|\widetilde{R}_j f\|_{L^1(dw)}$，适用于任意根系与非负重数函数。

Conclusion: 该结果建立了Dunkl-Schrödinger算子Hardy空间的Riesz变换刻画，推广了相关理论框架。

Abstract: Let $L=-Δ_k+V$ be a Schrödinger operator associated with the Dunkl Laplacian and a nonnegative potential. We characterize the Hardy space $H_L^1$, defined by the maximal operator of the semigroup generated by $-L$, in terms of the Riesz transforms associated with $L$. Under a local integral condition on the potential and a decay condition on the semigroup, formulated relative to an admissible covering by cubes, we prove that \[
  \|f\|_{H_L^1}
  \asymp
  \|f\|_{L^1(dw)}
  +\sum_{j=1}^N
  \|\widetilde{R}_j f\|_{L^1(dw)}. \] The proof combines an atomic characterization of $H_L^1$ with local Hardy space theory and weighted integral estimates for Dunkl derivatives of the heat kernels. The result applies to arbitrary root systems and nonnegative multiplicity functions.

</details>

Method: 设E_1、E_2为C^d上的两个算子空间结构，对应谱半径ρ_{E_1}与ρ_{E_2}在所有d元组算子上取值一致，通过比较两者诱导的完全范数来证明恒等映射的完全等距性质；并对最小谱半径作平行论证。

Result: 若ρ_{E_1}=ρ_{E_2}在所有d元组算子上成立，则id:(C^d,E_1)→(C^d,E_2)完全等距，从而E_1=E_2；最小谱半径情形亦有类似结论。

Abstract: We show that the spectral radius introduced by Shalit and Shamovich uniquely determines the underlying operator space structure: If E_1 and E_2 are operator space structures on C^d such that the corresponding spectral radii ρ_{E_1} and ρ_{E_2} agree on every d-tuple of operators, then the identity map is completely isometric. This answers a question of Scherer, Shalit and Shamovich. Moreover, we obtain an analogous result for the minimal spectral radii, and showcase an application to the completely bounded isomorphism problem for algebras of bounded non-commutative functions on operator space unit balls.

</details>

Abstract: We characterize extreme points of the (positive) unit ball of Marcinkiewicz spaces with respect to their natural quasi-norms. Having these results at hand, we show that every positive isometry on a noncommutative Marcinkiewicz space is of elementary form. In particular, a linear mapping on a noncommutative Marcinkiewicz space over an atomic von Neumann algebra with all atoms having the same trace is a positive surjective isometry if and only if it is the restriction of a Jordan $*$-isomorphism which preserves the singular value functions. We also study (not necessarily positive) surjective isometries on weak $L_p$-spaces affiliated a $σ$-finite non-atomic von Neumann algebra for $1<p<\infty$, Marcinkiewicz sequence spaces, and weak $\ell_p$ operator ideals, $p>0$, as well as one-parameter groups of isometries on the latter two.

</details>

Abstract: We study sparse Kahane--Salem--Zygmund constructions and weighted Hardy--Littlewood inequalities for homogeneous polynomials. For supports of cardinality $n^{d+o(1)}$, we determine the sharp power of $n$ governing the smallest norm of a unimodular $m$-linear form on $\ell_{p_1}^n\times\cdots\times\ell_{p_m}^n$; in the diagonal case, this yields the missing polynomial growth exponent in the coefficient-versus-supremum norm problem for $2\le p\le m$ and $2\le r\le\infty$. We then introduce a diagonal weighted Hardy--Littlewood functional which, on $s=p\ge m$, agrees exactly with the classical Hardy--Littlewood coefficient norm with the same optimal constant. We determine the optimal diagonal weight exponent for $2\le p\le m$ and $1\le q\le2$, on the full critical line $p=m$, and on a sharp part of the region $q>2$; at $q=\infty$ the optimal weight exponent is obtained for every $2\le p\le m$. The sparse coefficient estimates provide the matching dimensional obstructions.

</details>

Result: 主要结果表明，对每个有界对角算子D，对应的算子S可以几乎投影地通过D分解，分解常数为1^+，即任意接近1。作为推论，在规范投影满足自然有界性条件时，以及对所有赋予L^1范数的空间，均得到对角算子的分解结果。

Abstract: We study the Banach space and operator factorization structure of Janson-Sobolev and Janson-Sobolev-Hardy spaces. This new class of martingale spaces is determined by a $q$-adic filtration, a subspace $V\subset \mathbb{R}_0^{l\times q}$, and a rearrangement invariant function space $X$. Our main result shows that, for every bounded diagonal operator $D$, the operator $S = \sum_{t=1}^s λ_{\mathcal U}^{k_t}(D)Q_{\mathcal K_t}^{\mathcal B}$ determined by the linear functionals $λ_{\mathcal U}^{k_t}(D)$ and the canonical projections $Q_{\mathcal K_t}^{\mathcal B}$, almost projectionally factors through $D$ with constant $1^+$. As consequences, we obtain factorization results for diagonal operators both under a natural boundedness condition on the canonical projections and for all spaces equipped with the $L^1$-norm.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>

Conclusion: OQRW的弹道输运与势论回复性可由谱假设统一刻画，回复性呈现维数阈值现象，且可约与非齐性情形可通过h-变换与非齐性方法处理，为非交换量子游走的回复理论提供了框架。

Abstract: Our study connects root-mean-square ballistic transport to potential-theoretic recurrence for finite-range open quantum random walks. In homogeneous walks with primitive local channels, finitely many simple periodic Fourier peripheral eigenvalues, no nonzero stationary Fourier mode, and a nondegenerate quadratic spectral term, we prove a periodic uniform local limit theorem and recover exponential finite-set return bounds for nonzero drift. The centered case yields strong Green-function asymptotics in at least three dimensions, as well as potential-kernel asymptotics in one and two dimensions. According to these assumptions, the RMS ballistic speed equals the drift norm, nonzero drift indicates transience, and centered walks are recurring in effective dimensions one and two but transitory in higher dimensions. The low-dimensional finding shows a recurrence of the origin projection in TOM. For reducible walks, an explicit harmonic \(h\)-transform converts each absorption component to an OQRW, providing a detailed breakdown of Green occupation potentials. The squared RMS speed is the absorption-weighted mean of squared component drifts, but the reduced drift-dimension classification also needs specific component return estimations. A centered noncommuting family validates the fundamental spectral assumptions in all dimensions and provides explicit potential constants. Exact finite traps and sparse reflecting barriers provide a complementary nonhomogeneous method for zero speed and TOM recurrence.

</details>

Abstract: We develop a Banach-valued lateral Stieltjes calculus adapted to linear evolution equations on a Gelfand triple \[
  V\hookrightarrow H\simeq H^*\hookrightarrow V^*. \] The evolution problem is formulated with the posterior state, \[

The latter contains the exact positive jump contribution \[
  \frac12\sum_r\|u(r^+)-u(r)\|_H^2. \] Existence is constructed independently by Galerkin approximation and by a Rothe scheme adapted to the Stieltjes mass and its atoms. We also explain why the available Aubin--Lions theorem for anterior Stieltjes--Bochner evolution graphs is not automatically applicable to the posterior energy space and, in the present linear coercive setting, is not needed. A heat equation with Stieltjes time is included as a model application. The claims are positioned relative to earlier Stieltjes parabolic equations, general measure-driven evolution, and operator-valued-measure parabolic systems; no priority is claimed for measure-driven parabolic theory or Galerkin approximation in general.

Abstract: We study endpoint mapping properties of low-energy wave operators for Schrödinger operators $H=-Δ+V$ on $\mathbb R^n$, $n\ge3$. In dimension four, we prove that a zero-energy resonance prevents $L^1$ boundedness, whether or not zero is also an eigenvalue, under $|V(x)|\lesssim\langle x\rangle^{-β}$ with $β>10$. In particular, $L^\infty$ boundedness is equivalent to the vanishing of the zeroth, first, and harmonic second moments of $Vψ$ for every zero-energy eigenfunction $ψ$. The proof identifies the finite-rank obstruction and shows that the remaining eigenvalue correction cannot cancel its critical asymptotic profiles. Abstract: We construct a smooth solution of the focusing cubic nonlinear Schrödinger equation on a square two-dimensional torus which is global in both time directions and concentrates at one point as time tends to positive infinity. Its mass is the mass of the Euclidean ground state and its energy is zero. If $λ(t)$ denotes its concentration scale and $L$ the period, then $L/λ(t)=2\log t+\frac{11}{2}\log\log t+O(1)$. The solution approaches a phased, periodized ground state with an error tending to zero in every fixed Sobolev space. We also prove uniqueness, up to constant phase and time translation, within the quantitative single-profile asymptotic class used in the construction. The mechanism is the exponentially small interaction of the profile with its periodic images. Exact mass and energy constraints select the concentrating branch, while a positive energy on the moving constrained space controls the terminal-value problem at every fixed regularity order.

Conclusion: 构造的反例说明Kukavica与Kenig独立提出的猜想界在一般情况下不可达，同时确认 $C(1+\|V\|_\infty^{2/3})$ 是实值情形下消失阶数的sharp bound。

Abstract: Let $B_3=B(0,3)\subset\mathbb{R}^3$. Consequently, for every sufficiently large $N$, there is such a Dirichlet solution with potential norm smaller than $N$ and vanishing order at least $cN^{2/3}$. The construction extends directly to higher dimensions and to spheres. Together with previous results, this example indicates that $C(1+\|V\|_\infty^{2/3})$ is the sharp bound for the vanishing order in the real-valued case. It also indicates that the bound conjectured independently by Kukavica \cite{Kukavica1998} and Kenig \cite{Kenig2006} is not attainable in general.

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 约束保持L²范数，提供一致先验界，是高层能量估计的核心；对比无约束二维KS方程全局强解适定性尚未解决，探究约束能否带来全局适定性，并考察维数影响。

Method: 1D采用Fourier–Galerkin逼近与紧性论证；2D、3D用解析半群理论与Banach不动点定理证明局部强解存在唯一性；2D结合保持的L²范数、Agmon不等式与高阶能量估计控制非线性项。

Abstract: We study the Kuramoto--Sivashinsky (KS) equation in one, two, and three spatial dimensions subject to a codimension-one constraint. The constraint preserves the \(L^2\)-norm of the solution and consequently provides a uniform a priori bound that plays a central role in the higher-order energy estimates. In one spatial dimension, we establish global well-posedness by means of a Fourier--Galerkin approximation and compactness arguments. In two and three dimensions, we first prove the existence and uniqueness of local strong solutions using analytic semigroup theory and the Banach fixed-point theorem. In two dimensions, the preserved \(L^2\)-norm, combined with the Agmon inequality and suitable higher-order energy estimates, allows us to control the nonlinear terms and obtain global-in-time a priori bounds. Consequently, we establish the existence and uniqueness of global strong solutions for arbitrary sufficiently regular initial data, without any smallness assumption. This provides a global well-posedness result for the constrained two-dimensional KS equation, in contrast with the corresponding unconstrained problem, for which global well-posedness of strong solutions remains open in general. In three dimensions, although the constraint yields a uniform \(L^2\)-bound, the available interpolation and Agmon estimates are insufficient to close the higher-order energy estimate. This identifies a genuine dimension-dependent obstruction to extending the global regularity argument to three dimensions.

</details>


### [84] [Optimal Control of 2D Navier--Stokes Equations with Constraints of Finite Codimension](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00249)

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Abstract: We study an optimal control problem for the two-dimensional Navier-Stokes equations projected onto the tangent space of the $\mathbb{H}$-unit sphere manifold $\mathcal{M} := \{\boldsymbol{u} \in \mathbb{H} : \|\boldsymbol{u}\|_{\mathbb{H}}=1\}$. This geometric projection yields a constrained evolution system of finite codimension, ensuring that the state remains on $\mathcal{M}$ for all times. Using a Faedo-Galerkin approximation technique, we establish the global existence and uniqueness of strong solutions to the constrained Navier-Stokes equations with external controls $\boldsymbol{U} \in \mathrm{L}^2(0,T;\mathbb{H})$, together with uniform energy estimates. The associated optimal control problem, minimizing a quadratic cost functional subject to the manifold constraint, is shown to admit at least one optimal pair $(\boldsymbol{u}^*,\boldsymbol{U}^*)$. We derive the first-order necessary optimality conditions by formulating an optimal control system whose state-control pair $(\boldsymbol{u}^*,\boldsymbol{U}^*)$ evolves on the tangent bundle $T\mathcal{M}$ of the constraint manifold $\mathcal{M}$. In this setting, the control satisfies $\boldsymbol{U}^*(t)\in T_{\boldsymbol{u}^*(t)}\mathcal{M}$ for all $t\in[0,T]$, ensuring that the dynamics remain confined to the tangent bundle $T\mathcal{M}$. Furthermore, we establish the Fréchet differentiability of the control-to-state mapping $\boldsymbol{U}\mapsto \boldsymbol{u}_{\boldsymbol{U}}$ in $\mathrm{C}([0,T];\mathbb{H})\cap\mathrm{L}^2(0,T;\mathbb{V})$, with the corresponding linearized state characterized as the unique weak solution of a linearized constrained Navier-Stokes system preserving the tangency condition. These results provide a rigorous analytical foundation for the optimal control of fluid flows constrained to nonlinear manifolds.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非局部能量的小阶极限（如分数阶 Sobolev 范数在 $s\to 0$ 或 $s\to 1$ 时的渐近行为）是变分法与几何测度论中的重要问题，但已有结果多依赖欧氏空间结构与线性目标空间。作者希望在一般度量值映射、任意 Polish 目标空间上建立统一框架，覆盖 $p=1$ 端点，并统一解释 Maz'ya--Shaposhnikova 型公式与分数周长的概率刻画。

Method: 引入交互测度（interaction measures），假设其边际具有一致有界的 $L^\infty$ 密度 $a_\alpha,b_\alpha$。对全局 $L^p$ 映射，证明归一化能量收敛到以 $a_\infty+b_\infty$ 加权的 $L^p$ 能量，其充要条件是交互测度逃逸出任意有界矩形；对仅局部 $L^p$ 的映射，引入目标律（target laws）刻画无穷远处取值的分布，通过 $p$-距离剖面（$p$-distance profiles）的收敛得到极限交互能量，并以积分 $p$-Wasserstein 收敛作为充分判据。

Result: 在全局 $L^p$ 情形，得到能量收敛与交互测度逃逸有界矩形之间的等价刻画；在局部 $L^p$ 情形，对任意 $1\le p<\infty$ 与任意 Polish 目标空间证明极限交互能量存在并由 $p$-距离剖面收敛决定，积分 $p$-Wasserstein 收敛为其充分条件；所得结论包含端点 $p=1$，且不需要目标空间的线性结构。应用方面给出了方向性与各向异性 Maz'ya--Shaposhnikova 公式、分数周长的 Bernoulli 律解释，以及热半群能量的 Abelian 原理。

Conclusion: 该框架以交互测度与目标律为核心工具，统一处理度量值映射非局部能量的小阶极限，弱化了对目标空间线性结构和 $p>1$ 的限制，并将经典 Maz'ya--Shaposhnikova 型结果推广至方向性、各向异性及概率解释等新情形。

Abstract: We develop a measure-theoretic framework for small-order limits of nonlocal energies with metric-valued maps. For globally $L^p$ maps, we consider interaction measures whose marginals have uniformly bounded $L^\infty$ densities $a_α$ and $b_α$. If these densities converge weakly-* to $a_\infty$ and $b_\infty$, the normalized energies converge to the $L^p$ energy weighted by $a_\infty+b_\infty$ if and only if the interaction measures escape every bounded rectangle. For maps that are only locally $L^p$, we introduce target laws describing the distribution of the values sampled at infinity. Convergence of their $p$-distance profiles yields the limiting interaction energy for every $1\leq p<\infty$ and arbitrary Polish targets, with integrated $p$-Wasserstein convergence as a sufficient criterion. The arguments include the endpoint $p=1$ and require no linear structure on the target space. Applications include directional and anisotropic Maz'ya--Shaposhnikova formulas, a Bernoulli-law interpretation of fractional perimeters, and an Abelian principle for heat-semigroup energies.

</details>

Abstract: The one-dimensional planar compressible magnetohydrodynamic system is studied on unbounded domains. The longitudinal viscosity obeys a power law of the specific volume, which permits it to degenerate as the density approaches vacuum, and the heat conductivity follows a power law of the temperature. For any fixed non-negative exponent in the heat conductivity term, the existence and uniqueness of global strong solutions are established for arbitrarily large initial data, provided that the positive exponent for viscosity is sufficiently small. The specific volume and temperature possess time-independent upper and lower bounds. Moreover, for each admissible viscosity exponent, the solution converges uniformly in space to the equilibrium state as time tends to infinity.

Method: 提出抽象平均方法：将方程 $Mx=\varepsilon N(x,\varepsilon)$ 分解为一个平均相容性条件和一个非线性互补问题；主算子 $M$ 可为真正非线性算子。通过非退化条件、小Lipschitz条件（用于局部唯一性）以及可微情形下的局部 $C^1$ 分支分析，刻画解的存在性与结构，并推导解的领先渐近轮廓。

Abstract: We develop an abstract averaging method for equations $Mx=\varepsilon N(x,\varepsilon)$ in Banach spaces. The method applies when the equation can be separated into an averaged compatibility condition and a nonlinear complementary problem, and it allows the principal operator $M$ to be genuinely nonlinear. We prove that every nondegenerate zero of the corresponding averaged equation generates, for all sufficiently small nonzero values of $\varepsilon$, a nearby solution. Under a suitable small Lipschitz condition this solution is locally unique, while in the differentiable case it belongs to a unique local $C^1$ branch. The framework contains the abstract linear Fredholm averaging theory as a special case, but also covers quasilinear problems for which the principal operator has no invertible linearization at the reference state. We also determine the leading asymptotic profile of the solutions. Applications are given to a vector $p$-Laplacian Duffing system and to a nonsmooth problem in a Hilbert space.

### [88] [Uniqueness and nonuniqueness in the disjoint data inverse problem: a sharp spectral threshold](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00871)
*Lauri Ylinen*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文研究从波观测反演连通闭黎曼流形的唯一性问题，在亚临界谱下界条件下证明了刚性，并说明临界端点处刚性失效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 波观测数据通常无法覆盖整个流形，源与接收集可能相互分离，因此需要确定在何种条件下流形仍可被唯一恢复。已有唯一性结果依赖全局观测或较强的几何假设，本文旨在刻画谱集中性质对反问题唯一性的影响。

Method: 考虑源支集在开集S、波在开集R（可远离S）上观测的反问题。引入亚临界谱下界条件：对每个特征函数φ，有‖φ|_R‖_{L²(R)} ≥ c e^{-Cλ^θ}‖φ‖_{L²(M)}，其中θ∈[0,1/2)。在该类流形中证明唯一性；若谱下界对S亦成立，则推广到所有连通闭流形。通过构造反例处理θ=1/2的端点情形。

Result: 在满足亚临界谱下界（θ∈[0,1/2)）的流形类中，波观测唯一确定流形（至等距）。若S上也满足同类界，则唯一性在全体连通闭流形中成立。当θ=1/2时唯一性失效：对每个维数n≥2，构造出成对非等距连通闭n维黎曼流形，它们产生完全相同的波观测。

Conclusion: 谱集中性质的定量速率（亚临界指数θ<1/2）是波观测反问题唯一性的充分条件；而由谱不等式总可保证的临界指数θ=1/2处唯一性一般失效，表明该阈值是尖锐的而非技术性限制。

Abstract: We study the inverse problem of determining a connected, closed Riemannian manifold of dimension at least two from wave observations. The sources are supported in an open set $S$, and the resulting waves are observed on an open receiver set $R$, which may be at a positive distance from $S$. We prove uniqueness up to isometry within the class of manifolds that satisfy a subcritical spectral lower bound: $\|φ|_R\|_{L^2(R)} \ge ce^{-Cλ^θ}\|φ\|_{L^2(M)}$ for every eigenfunction $φ$, where $λ$ is the corresponding eigenvalue, $θ\in[0,1/2)$, and the constants $c,C>0$ do not depend on $φ$. If a bound of this type holds also on $S$, then uniqueness holds within the class of all connected, closed manifolds. By the spectral inequality, the bound always holds with $θ=1/2$. Uniqueness nevertheless fails at this endpoint: in every dimension $n\ge 2$, we construct a pair of nonisometric connected, closed Riemannian $n$-manifolds that produce identical wave observations.

</details>


### [89] [Derivative Embeddings in Vector-Valued Morrey Spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00292)
*Rishad Shahmurov,Veli Shahmurov*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文建立了 Banach 值演化类在时间 Morrey 控制下的中间导数理论：由 $u\in\mathcal M^{p,\lambda}(E_0)$ 与 $u^{(m)}\in\mathcal M^{p,\lambda}(E)$ 推出各中间导数的 Morrey 型嵌入，无需 UMD、Fourier 型或算子值乘子假设，并给出精确迹公式、紧性及 Hilbert 尺度改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典中间导数（插值）不等式通常依赖 UMD、Fourier 型或算子值乘子等假设，在一般 Banach 空间框架下受限。作者希望去掉这些假设，在时间方向仅具 Morrey 控制时建立普适的中间导数估计与迹定理。

Method: 核心手段包括：对 $D^j$ 作精确有理频率分裂、利用 Peetre $K$-泛函刻画插值空间 $(E_0,E)_{j/m,\infty}$、以及在 Morrey 空间上使用 Hardy--Littlewood 极大算子。有限区间情形构造 graph-Morrey 延拓；并结合精确 Morrey 迹定理。

Result: 得到无额外假设的嵌入 $u^{(j)}\in\mathcal M^{p,\lambda}((E_0,E)_{j/m,\infty})$；实直线上具尺度尖锐的乘法型估计；有限区间上同型嵌入；弱精细指标对任意 Banach 对最优。由导数定理与迹定理得到每个 $u^{(j)}$ 的精确满射迹公式、混合 Hölder/插值嵌入与迹驱动的 $p$-到-$q$ Morrey 增益。

Conclusion: 当 $E_0\hookrightarrow E$ 为紧嵌入时，上述嵌入在任意小损失插值光滑度的同 Morrey 范数下、以及在导数迹阈值之上的时间-Hölder 空间中均变为紧。对正自伴 Hilbert 尺度，可将弱插值目标改进为分数域 $D(B^{1-j/m})$，并证明该指数最优。

Abstract: We develop an intermediate-derivative theory for Banach-valued evolution classes with Morrey control in time. Let $E_0\hookrightarrow E$ be Banach spaces, $m\ge2$, $1<p<\infty$, and $0\leλ<1$. If $u\in\mathcal M^{p,λ}(E_0)$ and $u^{(m)}\in\mathcal M^{p,λ}(E)$, then every intermediate derivative satisfies \[ u^{(j)}\in\mathcal M^{p,λ}\bigl((E_0,E)_{j/m,\infty}\bigr),\qquad 0<j<m, \] with no UMD, Fourier type, or operator-valued multiplier assumption. The proof is based on an exact rational-frequency splitting of $D^j$, the Peetre $K$-functional, and the Hardy--Littlewood maximal operator on Morrey spaces. On the line the estimate has the scale-sharp multiplicative form. On finite intervals we construct a graph-Morrey extension and obtain the same embedding. The weak fine index is optimal for arbitrary Banach couples. Combining the derivative theorem with the exact Morrey trace theorem yields an exact onto trace formula for every $u^{(j)}$, together with mixed Hölder/interpolation embeddings and a trace-powered $p$-to-$q$ Morrey gain. If $E_0\hookc E$, these embeddings become compact both in the same Morrey norm after an arbitrarily small loss of interpolation smoothness and in time-Hölder spaces above the derivative-trace threshold. For positive selfadjoint Hilbert scales we further improve the weak interpolation target to the fractional domain $D(B^{1-j/m})$ and show that this exponent is sharp.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>

Conclusion: 该工作提供了拟线性微分方程周期问题的平均法扩展，给出了由平均方程非退化零点保证周期解局部唯一存在的抽象定理，并展示了在无穷维相对论受迫摆耦合系统中的应用。

Abstract: The aim of the paper is to provide an extension of the averaging method to the periodic problem for quasilinear differential equations $(φ(t,u'))' = \varepsilon f(t,u,u',\varepsilon)$, $u(0) = u(T)$, $u'(0) = u'(T)$, where $u$ and $u'$ take values in a Banach space $X$ and $φ$ is a suitable homeomorphism between a neighborhood of $0$ in $X$ and $X$. The approach follows from a given extension of the averaging method to first order systems of the form $u' = g(t,v)$, $v' = h(t,u,v)$, $u(0) = u(T)$, $v(0) = v(T)$, in a product of Banach spaces, obtained from a direct application of the implicit function theorem in Banach spaces to an equivalent problem. We prove that every nondegenerate zero of the corresponding averaged equation generates, for sufficiently small nonzero values of the parameter, a unique nearby periodic solution. An application is given to an infinite system of coupled forced pendulums with relativistic acceleration.

</details>


### [91] [Critical anisotropic $p$-Laplace equations without finite energy: classification and a Schoen-type Harnack inequality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00337)
*Phuong Le*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文在无有限能量、可积性或渐近性假设条件下，对一般（可能非可逆）Minkowski范数下的各向异性p-Laplace临界方程的非负局部弱解进行了完整分类：每个非零解都是各向异性Sobolev气泡；同时证明了尺度不变的Schoen型Harnack不等式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Antonini–Ciraolo–Gatti提出的Problem A（以及其非能量各向异性版本）此前仅在Euclidean情形由Zhang解决，各向异性情形则受p范围限制（Chen–Wu–Yan–Yang）。本文旨在对全范围1<p<N给出各向异性类比，并去除已有工作对p的限制，且不施加有限能量/可积性/渐近假设。

Result: 所有非负局部弱解被完整分类，非零解均为各向异性Sobolev气泡；获得尺度不变的Schoen型Harnack不等式；且结论对全范围1<p<N成立，无需有限能量、可积性或渐近假设。

Conclusion: 该工作在全范围1<p<N上解决了Antonini–Ciraolo–Gatti Problem A的各向异性类比（Euclidean情形由Zhang完成），并去除了Chen–Wu–Yan–Yang非能量各向异性分类中对p的限制。

Abstract: Let $N\ge 2$ and $1<p<N$. Let $H$ be a possibly non-reversible Minkowski norm on $\mathbb{R}^N$, with $H\in C^2(\mathbb{R}^N\setminus\{0\})$ and $D^2(H^2/2)$ uniformly positive definite away from the origin. We classify all nonnegative local weak solutions $u\in W^{1,p}_{\rm loc}(\mathbb{R}^N)$ of \[ -\operatorname{div}\big(H(\nabla u)^{p-1}\nabla H(\nabla u)\big)=u^{p^*-1},\qquad p^*=\frac{Np}{N-p}, \] without any finite-energy, integrability, or asymptotic assumption. Every nonzero solution is an anisotropic Sobolev bubble. This settles, for the full range $1<p<N$, the anisotropic analogue of Problem A of Antonini--Ciraolo--Gatti, whose Euclidean case was settled by Zhang, and removes the restriction on $p$ in the non-energy anisotropic classification of Chen--Wu--Yan--Yang. We also prove a scale-invariant Schoen-type Harnack inequality for local solutions.
  The proof follows the strategy of Zhang for the Euclidean $p$-Laplacian, combining rigidity of bounded blow-up profiles and a first-contact argument with the integral bootstrap of Ciraolo and Gatti. The anisotropic analysis requires two additional ingredients. First, we establish a Bochner identity for the conjugated stress tensor in distributional form on the noncritical set, using only $C^2$ regularity of $H$. Second, we prove a two-term expansion at isolated singularities of Finsler $p$-harmonic functions, including the gradient control needed by the Pohozaev invariant. For non-reversible anisotropies the argument is formulated with the reflected norm. A Morrey-type estimate proved directly for the relevant Leray--Lions operator replaces the potential-theoretic input used in the Euclidean argument.

</details>


### [92] [Global weak varifold solutions to Navier-Stokes/Mean Curvature Flow system](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00732)

Motivation: Navier-Stokes/平均曲率流系统描述由尖锐界面分隔的两相不可压缩、黏性、不互溶流体，界面演化由对流平均曲率流方程控制并耦合表面张力项，可刻画干泡沫动力学，并作为非消失迁移率下Navier-Stokes/Allen-Cahn系统的尖锐界面极限出现。尽管应用广泛，其任意类型弱解的全局时间存在性仍是具有挑战性的公开问题。
