Result: 首次证明了此类弱解的无条件全局时间存在性；并证明Navier-Stokes/平均曲率流系统的任意经典解在该新varifold弱解类中是唯一的。

Conclusion: 该工作通过提出新的varifold弱解框架，解决了Navier-Stokes/平均曲率流系统长期存在的全局弱解存在性问题，同时以相对熵方法保证了经典解在该弱解类中的唯一性，为相关两相流模型的分析提供了新工具。

Abstract: The Navier-Stokes/Mean Curvature Flow system describes a two-phase flow of incompressible, viscous and immiscible fluids separated by a sharp interface, whose evolution is governed by a convective mean curvature flow equation, coupled to a two-phase Navier-Stokes equation accounting for surface tension. Among others, this model describes the dynamics of dry foams and it also arises as a sharp interface limit of Navier-Stokes/Allen-Cahn systems with non-vanishing mobility. Despite this wide range of applications, global-in-time existence of any kind of weak solutions remains a challenging open problem. In this work, we introduce a novel notion of varifold weak solution in two and three ambient dimensions, based on a sharp energy dissipation principle à la De Giorgi, and we show for the first time the unconditional global-in-time existence of such a weak solution. We also show, by means of a relative entropy approach, that any classical solution to the Navier-Stokes/Mean Curvature Flow system is unique in the class of our new weak varifold solutions.

Abstract: We summarize recent developments in the theory of the Benjamin-Ono equation, a well-known long-wave asymptotic model for internal waves in deep water, focusing on results obtained by means of an explicit formula for the solution of the Cauchy problem in terms of given initial data. We give a full description of this formula, and provide simple examples, with the aim of bringing it into the realm of applied mathematics. We also show how the formula simplifies further upon restriction to rational initial data. We then show how the formula and its rational restriction explain in great detail, and in a strikingly simple fashion, the asymptotic behavior of solutions of the Benjamin-Ono equation in the small-dispersion and long-time limits. In the setting of long-time asymptotics, we prove some new results related to soliton resolution yielding pointwise convergence with a concrete decay rate under the assumption of rational initial data. The first half of the paper is a stand-alone general survey and user's guide, while the appendices constituting the second half are intended for readers who want to explore the topics in greater depth, and it includes the proofs of our new results.

Abstract: We study finite-time blow-up and global existence for semilinear heat equations with time-dependent reaction coefficients on metric measure spaces. For convex nonlinearities, we establish a blow-up criterion that retains the dependence on the initial datum through its linear evolution and does not require stochastic completeness. Under additional conservativity and heat kernel assumptions, we also prove blow-up at the critical Fujita exponent associated with polynomial volume growth. Our main global existence result is based on a heat-kernel-weighted functional space in which the mild solution map becomes a contraction. This yields a unified construction covering both polynomial and exponential heat kernel decay, general locally Lipschitz nonlinearities, and time-dependent reaction coefficients, without convexity or monotonicity assumptions. In particular, the global existence criterion only uses the behaviour of the nonlinearity on the range explored by the solution. Applications include Riemannian manifolds, metric graphs, and, within a separate discrete framework, weighted graphs. The arguments, especially the functional construction underlying global existence, are new and lead to new results even for the classical underlying spaces considered above.

Abstract: Let $K\subset\mathbb{R}^n$ be a convex body, let $X$ and $Y$ be independent uniform points of $K$, and set $R_K=(|K|/ω_n)^{1/n}$. We consider the distribution function \[
  D_K(ρ)=\mathbb P\{|X-Y|/R_K<ρ\}, \] and its homogeneous counterpart \begin{equation*}
  \mathcal J_ρ(K)=\iint_{K\times K}\mathbf{1}_{\{|x-y|<ρR_K\}}dxdy. \end{equation*} Thus $D_K(ρ)$ is the proportion of ordered pairs of points of $K$ whose distance is less than $ρR_K$, or equivalently the radial distribution function of the normalized covariogram of $K$.
  The functional satisfies a Brunn--Minkowski inequality, with equality precisely for homothetic bodies, and its differential gives a first Minkowski inequality. We characterize the Borel measures that occur as Wulff differentials: they are exactly the nonzero finite positive measures with zero centroid which are not concentrated on a great subsphere, and the corresponding body is unique up to translation. We further prove fixed-volume and stationary rigidity of the ball in the subcritical range, together with regularity for the resulting equation. At saturation, the equation is the classical Minkowski equation.

</details>

Abstract: This paper studies the focusing Calogero--Moser derivative nonlinear Schrödinger (CMdNLS) equation under the nonzero boundary condition \(|u(t,x)|\to 1\) as \(|x|\to+\infty\). In sharp contrast to the classical paradigm for the cubic nonlinear Schrödinger equation, namely that focusing nonlinearities support only bright solitons on a zero background, we show that the focusing CMdNLS equation admits a rich family of \emph{dark multi-soliton} solutions on a nonzero background, whose existence is driven by a spectral gap \([-1,0]\) in the point spectrum of the Lax operator, together with the Hardy space structure.
  The main results are threefold. First, we establish an explicit formula for general solutions in the Hardy--Zhidkov space \(\mathcal Z^2_+\), via a \emph{semigroup method} based on new commutator estimates between the Lax--Beurling shift semigroup and Toeplitz operators. This approach circumvents the non-commutativity of the unbounded generator with the time derivative and requires no weighted hypothesis on solutions or on the domain of validity of commutator formulas. Second, the algebraic definition of dark multi-soliton potentials is shown to be equivalent to two spectral conditions on the Lax operator. Together with the conservation of a generating functional, this yields the invariance of the dark \(N\)-soliton set \(\mathcal U_N\) under the CMdNLS flow. Third, the action-angle variables on \(\mathcal U_N\) are constructed, which establishes the complete integrability of the focusing CMdNLS equation on \(\mathcal U_N\).
  Combining these results, we obtain an inverse spectral formula, from which it follows that the poles of dark multi-soliton solutions satisfy a complexified Calogero--Moser system. Long time asymptotics and uniform Sobolev estimates are also derived.

</details>

Abstract: We study the critical points $U_\varepsilon$ of the two-dimensional Ginzburg--Landau energy on domains with corners $Ω$ and imposed Dirichlet boundary condition which vanishes precisely at the vertices $V$. We isolate the forced scalar boundary layer $ρ_\varepsilon$ by the weighted Lassoued--Mironescu splitting. Assuming the reduced logarithmic energy bound and vertex logarithmic tightness, we prove that after passing to a subsequence, the normalized maps $v_\varepsilon = U_\varepsilon/ρ_\varepsilon$ converges in $C^k_{\mathrm{loc}}(Ω\setminus A)$ $\forall k\ge0$ away from a finite set of interior vortices $A$. The limiting map is a canonical $S^1$-valued harmonic map $u_*$ containing integer interior vortex factors and fixed fractional corner factors. The renormalized energy is defined by removing discs around the interior vortices and sectors around the vertices and subtracting the corresponding logarithmic divergences. The interior vortex locations of the limiting map are critical points of this renormalized energy.

Abstract: We study an incompressible visco-morphoelastic system in three dimensions in which the Eulerian effective strain is transported by the Zaremba-Jaumann rate and coupled to a Navier-Stokes momentum balance through a Kelvin-Voigt stress. The strain remains symmetric and splits into a scalar transport-relaxation equation for its trace and a deviatoric equation containing the nonlinear stretching and Jaumann terms. We regularize both equations by diffusion of order $\varepsilon$, which admits a mechanical interpretation as weakly nonlocal remodeling with a characteristic length scale. In contrast, the Jaumann commutator is a product of two weakly convergent sequences, and its limit cannot be identified from the available estimates. The energy-level cancellation used in related corotational viscoelastic models does not close here because the coefficient of the stretching term is itself an unknown.

</details>

Abstract: We establish a Pogorelov type interior $C^2$ estimate for the real twisted Monge-Ampere equation without assuming uniform ellipticity. This equation is fully nonlinear and elliptic, but is neither concave nor a function of the eigenvalues of the Hessian, so the standard techniques for interior second-derivative estimates do not directly apply. Building on the partial Legendre transform formulation of Streets and Warren, we derive a differential identity for the associated positive-definite matrix $W(D^2u)$ and use some linear algebra to control the third-order terms arising in the maximum principle argument. As an application, we prove a Liouville theorem for entire split convex solutions with quadratic growth, extending the rigidity theorem of Streets and Warren by replacing their uniform ellipticity hypothesis with suitable quadratic growth assumptions.

Abstract: We establish a global uniqueness result at the critical Sobolev regularity for the isotropic Calderón problem in all dimensions $n\ge 4$. ### [101] [Sharp higher-order uncertainty principles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01561)
*Hongtao Hu,Meiqi Liu,Wenming Zou*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文建立了一类与不确定性原理相关的尖锐高阶不等式，包括高阶Heisenberg不确定性原理的简洁证明、无旋向量场情形，以及不含高阶张量和径向对称函数的相关结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 围绕不确定性原理的高阶推广，已有工作缺乏对最优常数与极值函数存在性的系统刻画，且Maz'ya提出的高阶开问题尚未解决，因此需要建立统一的尖锐高阶不等式框架。

Method: 采用"展开平方"（expanding the squares）方法，给出尖锐高阶Heisenberg不确定性原理的简洁证明，并显式给出最优常数与极值函数的存在性；将该方法推广至无旋向量场情形。

Result: 得到了尖锐高阶Heisenberg不确定性原理及其最优常数和极值函数；获得了无旋向量场的尖锐高阶不等式，并在N=2时回答了Maz'ya开问题的高阶版本；此外建立了不含高阶张量的尖锐高阶不等式，以及径向对称函数的高阶Heisenberg与高阶氢原子不确定性原理。

Conclusion: 该工作系统推进了高阶不确定性原理的尖锐化理论，解决了公开问题，并拓展到向量场、无张量形式与径向对称函数等情形。

Abstract: We establish a class of sharp higher-order inequalities related to the uncertainty principle. First, by means of the expanding the squares method, we give a concise proof of sharp higher-order Heisenberg uncertainty principles. We provide explicit expressions for the optimal constants and establish the existence of extremal functions. Furthermore, we obtain sharp higher-order Heisenberg uncertainty principles for curl-free vector fields. In particular, when $N=2$, we give an answer to the higher-order version of Open Problem 9 raised by Maz'ya in [Integr. Equ. Oper. Theory (2018) 90:25]. Second, we establish sharp higher-order Heisenberg uncertainty principles that do not involve higher-order tensors. We also prove sharp higher-order Heisenberg uncertainty principles and higher-order hydrogen uncertainty principles for radially symmetric functions.

</details>


### [102] [The anisotropic Calderón problem for Riemannian metrics at high fixed frequency](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01280)
*Mihajlo Cekić,Katya Krupchyk,Suman Kumar Sahoo,Gunther Uhlmann*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 该文研究带严格凸边界的紧致非捕获黎曼流形上Helmholtz方程的反边界值问题，证明在固定且足够大的频率下，Dirichlet-to-Neumann映射相等蕴含透镜数据在边界固定微分同胚意义下相等，并给出定量版本，从而建立Calderón问题与透镜刚性之间的高频桥梁。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 建立Calderón问题（由Dirichlet-to-Neumann映射恢复系数/度量）与透镜刚性（由散射关系恢复度量）之间的高频联系，并理解高频边界测量中蕴含的几何信息。

Method: 构造沿极大测地线集中的Gaussian beam型解，其在边界附近分裂为出射/入射部分，出射部分的相位编码出射点、方向与旅行时间；随后通过驻相分析与边界积分恒等式恢复透镜数据，并给出在合适有界度量集上一致成立的定量版本。

Result: 对于足够大但固定的频率λ，两个度量若Dirichlet-to-Neumann映射相等，则其透镜数据在相差一个边界固定的光滑微分同胚意义下相等；该结论具有在度量有界集上一致的定量形式。

Conclusion: 在非捕获、严格凸边界的黎曼流形上，高频Helmholtz方程的Dirichlet-to-Neumann数据足以确定透镜数据，从而在Calderón问题与透镜刚性之间架起高频桥梁。

Abstract: We study an inverse boundary value problem for the Helmholtz equation on a smooth compact non-trapping Riemannian manifold with strictly convex boundary. We prove that, given two such metrics, for sufficiently large but fixed frequency $λ$, equality of their Dirichlet-to-Neumann maps implies equality of their lens data, up to a smooth boundary-fixing diffeomorphism. This establishes a high-frequency bridge between the Calderón problem and lens rigidity. We also prove a quantitative version of this result uniformly valid in a suitable bounded set of Riemannian metrics. Crucially, we show that Gaussian beam type solutions concentrating on maximal geodesics, split near the boundary into outgoing/incoming parts, such that the phase of the outgoing part contains information on exit point, direction, and travel time. The recovery of lens data then proceeds by a careful stationary phase analysis and a boundary integral identity.

</details>


### [103] [The Allen-Cahn equation and general minimal cones](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01335)
*Oscar Ivan Agudelo Rico,Matteo Rizzi*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 在 $\mathbb{R}^{N+1}$ ($N\ge3$) 中构造 Allen-Cahn 方程 $\Delta u+u-u^3=0$ 的解，使其零水平集在无穷远处渐近于给定极小锥，并计算其 Morse 指标；该构造仅需非退化性假设，不要求锥的稳定性或对称性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推广 Pacard 和 Wei (2013) 关于高维 Allen-Cahn 方程稳定解的结果。原结果要求底锥为面积最小化或至少稳定；本文旨在回答 Pacard-Wei 提出的问题，即能否放宽对底锥的这一假设。

Method: 利用非退化性假设（无需稳定性或对称性）构造解，使零水平集在无穷远处渐近于给定极小锥，并通过分析计算解的 Morse 指标。

Result: 在仅依赖非退化性假设的条件下，成功构造出零水平集渐近于给定极小锥的解，并计算出其 Morse 指标。作为推论，还得到具有无穷 Morse 指标且零水平集连通的解。

Conclusion: 本文正面回答了 Pacard 和 Wei 提出的关于放宽底锥假设的问题，将原结果从稳定/面积最小化锥推广到仅需非退化性的更一般情形。

Abstract: We construct solutions to the Allen-Cahn equation $Δu+u-u^3=0$ in $\mathbb{R}^{N+1}$, with $N\ge 3$, whose zero level set is asymptotic (at infinity) to a given minimal cone. Our construction is quite general, since we only use a suitable nondegeneracy assumption; neither stability nor symmetry of the underlying cone is required. Moreover, we calculate the morse index of such solutions. In particular, our main result generalizes a result by Pacard and Wei (2013) dealing with stable solutions to the Allen-Cahn equation in high dimensions. More precisely, we give a positive answer to a remark raised in the paper by Pacard and Wei about the possibility to relax the assumption for the underlying cone is area-minimizing or at least stable. As a corollary, we also obtain solutions with infinite morse index and connected zero level set.

</details>


### [104] [On regularity of averaged Green's functions in homogenization of elliptic PDE](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01656)
*Joseph G. Conlon,Michael Dabkowski*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文研究 $\mathbb{Z}^d$ 上具有随机系数的均匀椭圆散度型偏差分方程，利用 Bourgain 的洞见，在非对角情形下建立了平均格林函数二阶导数的估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有结果证明平均格林函数在大尺度下可被均匀化格林函数逐点良好逼近，且对平均格林函数直至（但不包括）二阶导数的（分数阶）导数也有相应结果。二阶导数结果此前仅在椭圆方程生成正半群（如对角情形）时成立，本文旨在将二阶导数结果推广到更一般的情形。

Method: 采用 Bourgain 的一个洞见，对 $\mathbb{Z}^d$ 上均匀椭圆散度型随机系数偏差分方程的平均格林函数进行二阶导数估计。

Result: 在更一般（不要求生成正半群）的情形下证明了平均格林函数二阶导数的估计结果。

Conclusion: 借助 Bourgain 的洞见，成功将平均格林函数二阶导数的逼近结果从对角（正半群）情形推广到一般均匀椭圆散度型偏差分方程情形。

Abstract: This paper is concerned with establishing estimates on averaged Green's functions for a uniformly elliptic divergence form partial difference equation with random coefficients on the $d$ dimensional integer lattice $\mathbb{Z}^d$. It has previously been shown that the averaged Green's function is point-wise well approximated by the homogenized Green's function at large length scales. Corresponding results also hold for (fractional) derivatives of the averaged Green's function up to but not including the second derivative. Second derivative results have been established when the elliptic equation generates a positive semi-group, as in the diagonal case. Here the second derivative result is shown more generally by using an insight of Bourgain.

</details>


### [105] [Local separation profiles for the stationary Prandtl system](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01689)
*Ky Giao Duong,Tej-Eddine Ghoul,Sameer Iyer,Yasunori Maekawa*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文在常逆压梯度下构造二维定常Prandtl系统的解，得到一族不同的分离律；并对ℓ=1的平方根分离情形证明其动态稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决Oleinik与Samokhin提出的Open Problem 5，刻画分离点附近解的局部渐近结构，并厘清分离律的多样性与稳定性。

Method: 在von Mises变量下围绕基态进行线性化，结合内、外展开的匹配渐近分析构造解。

Result: 对每个整数ℓ≥1，构造光滑初值使分离在有限位置x=x*发生，且∂_y u(x,0)=C_ℓ(x*-x)^{ℓ/2}(1+o(1))；并证明ℓ=1的平方根分离在适当拓扑下对小扰动稳定。

Conclusion: Prandtl系统可在一族分离律下实现有限位置分离，其中平方根分离具有动态稳定性。

Abstract: We construct solutions to the two-dimensional stationary Prandtl system under a constant adverse pressure gradient exhibiting a family of distinct separation laws. More precisely, for every integer $\ell\ge1$, we construct smooth initial data for which separation occurs at a finite location $x=x^\ast$, with \[ \partial_y u(x,0) =\mathbf C_\ell(x^\ast-x)^{\ell/2}\bigl(1+o(1)\bigr), \qquad x\uparrow x^\ast, \] where $\mathbf C_\ell>0$ depends on the initial data.
  We determine the local asymptotic structure of the solution near separation through matched inner and outer expansions based on linearization around the ground state in von Mises variables. This description addresses Open Problem~5 posed by Oleinik and Samokhin~\cite{OleinikSamok-book-99}. We also prove that the square-root separation regime, corresponding to $\ell=1$, is dynamically stable under small admissible perturbations of the initial data in a suitable topology.

</details>


### [106] [Nonlinear stability of the Guderley imploding shock wave in radial symmetry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01690)
*Giorgio Cialdea*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 在三维可压缩欧拉方程（绝热范围 $1<\gamma\leq 5/3$）中，证明了 Guderley 内爆激波在径向扰动下的非线性稳定性，扰动解在有限时间内形成渐近自相似内爆。允许激波前方静止区存在小的非零常数压力。证明结合了沿特征线的加权 $L^\infty$ 估计与 Guderley 剖面稳定性不等式的计算机辅助证明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Guderley 内爆激波是有限时间内自相似奇点形成的经典模型，其非线性稳定性是理解内爆奇点普适性的核心问题。已有结果多局限于线性分析或特定情形，缺乏对含背景压力扰动的一般非线性稳定性论证，因此需要建立严格的非线性稳定性理论。

Method: 采用沿特征线的加权 $L^\infty$ 估计方法来控制扰动演化，并结合针对 Guderley 自相似剖面的稳定性不等式进行计算机辅助证明，以处理三维径向扰动下的非线性耦合。

Result: 证明了在绝热范围 $1<\gamma\leq 5/3$ 下 Guderley 内爆激波在径向扰动下的非线性稳定性；扰动解在有限时间内发展为渐近自相似内爆；结果允许激波前方静止区带小的非零常数压力扰动。

Conclusion: 该工作为可压缩欧拉方程内爆奇点的非线性稳定性提供了严格证明，将稳定性分析从线性层面推广到含背景压力的非线性情形，并展示了加权 $L^\infty$ 估计与计算机辅助验证相结合的有效性。

Abstract: We prove nonlinear stability of the Guderley imploding shock under radial perturbations for the three-dimensional compressible Euler equations in the adiabatic range $1<γ\leq\frac{5}{3}$. The perturbed solutions develop an asymptotically self-similar implosion in finite time. Our result allows perturbations with small, nonzero constant pressure in the quiescent region ahead of the shock. The proof combines weighted $L^\infty$ estimates along characteristics with a computer-assisted proof of stability inequalities for the Guderley profile.

</details>


### [107] [Regularity for solutions to inhomogeneous degenerate parabolic $p$-Laplace equations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01724)
*Xia Hao,Yan Li,Zhiwen Zhao*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文研究非齐次退化抛物型p-Laplace方程弱解的正则性，通过建立时间Lipschitz估计，直接导出水平集空间测度的前向传播，绕开传统能量不等式方法，并获得时空W^{1,∞}_{x,t}估计及正等待时间条件的反例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对p-Laplace型拟线性退化抛物方程弱解，应用内蕴尺度法证明Hölder正则性的核心障碍在于：推导水平集空间测度的前向时间传播估计。本文旨在重新审视并克服这一困难。

Method: 针对带非负有界、时间无关强迫项及初值的非齐次退化抛物p-Laplace方程，建立任意与初始时刻相隔正距离的时间区间上的时间Lipschitz估计。利用该估计提供的强正则性增益，直接推导水平集测度的前向传播，从而避开作为先前方法起点的能量不等式。

Result: 建立了时间Lipschitz估计；由此直接导出水平集测度的前向传播；进一步获得远离初始时刻的时空W^{1,∞}_{x,t}估计；并构造反例表明在仅有界初值条件下正等待时间条件的尖锐性。

Conclusion: 通过时间Lipschitz估计可绕过能量不等式直接得到水平集前向传播，为内蕴尺度法提供新途径，并证明正等待时间条件在仅有界初值下的最优性。

Abstract: For weak solutions to quasilinear degenerate parabolic equations of $p$-Laplace type, a central obstacle in applying the method of intrinsic scaling to prove their Hölder regularity is the derivation of forward-in-time propagation estimate for the spatial measure of level sets. In this paper, we revisit and overcome this difficulty for an inhomogeneous degenerate parabolic $p$-Laplace equation with bounded nonnegative time-independent forcing term and initial data. Our new proof is built upon the temporal Lipschitz estimate that we establish on any time interval separated from the initial time by a positive amount. The strong regularity gain furnished by this estimate allows us to derive the forward propagation of level-set measures directly, thereby bypassing the energy inequality that served as the starting point of the previous approach. Furthermore, we establish space-time $W^{1,\infty}_{x,t}$ estimates away from the initial time and construct counterexamples to demonstrate the sharpness of the positive waiting-time condition under merely bounded initial data.

</details>


### [108] [Inverse Energy Cascade and Non-uniqueness for the Advection-Diffusion Equation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01779)
*Alexey Cheskidov,Xiaoran Liu*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文证明了对流扩散方程在低于 $L_t^2L_x^\infty$ 的对数近临界漂移类中解的非唯一性，并刻画了 $L^{2,\infty}_tL^\infty_x$ 漂移下能量不等式解的唯一性边界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究对流扩散方程在近临界漂移正则性下的适定性边界，明确唯一性成立与失效的临界范围。

Method: 构造两类反例：其一为扩散辅助的标量能量逆级联，在 $t\to0$ 时发散；其二为压缩逆混合，扩散仅作微扰，得到初始时刻能量跳变的有界抛物解。漂移与标量在正时间光滑。另在 $L^{2,\infty}_tL^\infty_x$ 漂移及几乎处时刻起满足能量不等式的设定下进行分析。

Result: 在低于 $L_t^2L_x^\infty$ 的对数近临界类中构造出非唯一解。在 $L^{2,\infty}_tL^\infty_x$ 漂移、从几乎处时刻满足能量不等式的设定下，证明有界能量解唯一；同时构造出能量在 $t\to0$ 时爆破的非唯一解。

Conclusion: 唯一性阈值位于 $L_t^2L_x^\infty$ 附近：略低于该类的近临界漂移可导致非唯一性，而 $L^{2,\infty}_tL^\infty_x$ 下有界能量解仍保持唯一，非唯一性仅在能量无界时出现。

Abstract: We prove non-uniqueness for the advection-diffusion equation with a drift in a logarithmically near-critical class below $L_t^2L_x^\infty$. The first construction is a diffusion-assisted inverse cascade of scalar energy that diverges as $t \to 0$. The second uses compressed inverse mixing, with diffusion acting perturbatively, and produces a bounded parabolic solution with the energy jump at the initial time. In both constructions the drift and scalar are smooth for positive time. We also consider the drift in $L^{2,\infty}_tL^\infty_x$ and solutions satisfying the energy inequality starting from almost every time. In this setting, we prove uniqueness of solutions with bounded energy, and construct non-unique solution with energy blowing up as time goes to zero.

</details>


### [109] [Resonance expansions and local-energy decay estimates for Dirac operators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02108)
*Zhuo Chen,Michael Melgaard*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 该文研究三维半经典Dirac算子的共振展开与局域长时间动力学，在解析微扰下建立指数截断预解估计、共振展开，并在无陷能量窗给出局域能量快速衰减及显式衰减阈值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究半经典Dirac算子共振与长时间局域动力学，刻画正负能量共振对传播子的时间方向贡献，并量化局域能量衰减速率。

Method: 指数截断预解界与薄无共振矩形内的多项式界；共振数局部上界O(h^{-3})与鸽笼选围道；几乎解析函数演算与Cauchy–Green形变；对数无共振区、定量FBI变换/逃逸函数论证；有限时间传播；最大正叶连接时间T_χ与几何预解估计。

Result: 得到谱局域传播子的共振展开：正能量共振贡献大正时间，负能量共振贡献大负时间；质量型微扰在无陷能量窗给出连续截断预解多项式界与局域能量快速衰减，因衰减阈值T_N>T_χ+(N+1)/M。

Conclusion: 对完整畸变预解给出均匀[h log(1/h)]^{-1}界的严格微局域障碍，并识别出对数深度下夹层连续预解的有限飞行时间放大机制。

Abstract: We study resonance expansions and localised long-time dynamics for three-dimensional semiclassical Dirac operators. For smooth Hermitian matrix-valued perturbations which are analytic outside a compact set, we first obtain exponential cut-off resolvent bounds away from the resonance set and polynomial bounds in thin resonance-free rectangles adjacent to the real axis. Combining these estimates with a local $\Oc(h^{-3})$ upper bound on the number of resonances, a pigeonhole selection of admissible contours, almost-analytic functional calculus, and a Cauchy--Green deformation, we derive resonance expansions for spectrally localised propagators. Positive-energy resonances contribute for large positive times, whereas negative-energy resonances contribute for large negative times. We then specialise to mass-type perturbations $\mbfβV(x)$ in a nontrapping energy window. Using the logarithmic resonance-free region established in our earlier work together with a quantitative FBI-transform/escape-function argument, we obtain a polynomial bound for the continued cut-off resolvent and deduce rapid local-energy decay. We subsequently refine this polynomial estimate by a finite-time propagation argument in the undistorted region. Introducing the maximal positive-sheet connection time $T_χ$ associated with the spatial cutoff, we prove a geometric continued-resolvent estimate and, for every fixed $M$ below the resonance-free depth constant, derive the explicit local-energy decay threshold $T_N>T_χ+(N+1)/M$. The same analysis gives a rigorous microlocal obstruction to a uniform $[h\log(1/h)]^{-1}$ bound for the full distorted resolvent and identifies a finite-flight-time amplification mechanism for the sandwiched continued resolvent at logarithmic depth.

</details>


### [110] [On Two Species Long Range Segregation in an Annular Domain](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02141)
*Howen Chuah*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文研究环状区域上K=2种群的长程分离椭圆方程组，利用解的唯一性与旋转不变性证明自由边界由同心球面组成且唯一确定，并分析自由边界随区域、相互作用距离和边界数据的变化，导出自由边界半径的ODE。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该方程组用于模拟种群的长程分离现象，已有工作分别研究了二维自由边界的正则性（\cite{CL2}）及高维自由边界的部分正则性（\cite{ChPaTo26_2}, \cite{ChPaTo26_3}），但对环状区域中K=2种群的自由边界几何结构与定量依赖关系尚未明确。

Method: 在任意维数环状区域中考虑K=2种群的特殊情形，利用解的唯一性与旋转不变性刻画自由边界几何；应用\cite{CL2}导出的自由边界条件证明自由边界的唯一性；研究自由边界半径对区域、相互作用距离和边界数据的依赖性；并针对域、相互作用距离、边界数据均依赖于t的单参数族，分别在二维与n≥3维导出自由边界半径的ODE。

Result: 证明自由边界由同心球面构成，且其唯一确定；给出自由边界半径随区域、相互作用距离和边界数据的变化规律，特别地，当相互作用距离趋于零时自由边界收敛到相邻模型的自由边界；分别给出二维与n≥3维的自由边界半径ODE，并讨论了若干示例。

Conclusion: 在环状区域K=2种群情形下，长程分离椭圆方程组的自由边界具有同心球面的简单几何结构且唯一，其半径可通过ODE刻画并随参数连续变化，在相互作用距离趋于零时与相邻模型一致。

Abstract: We consider a system of elliptic equations, depending on a small parameter $ε> 0$, which models the long range segregation of populations. The system has been previously studied in \cite{CL2} for the regularity of the free boundary in dimension $2$ and in \cite{ChPaTo26_2} and \cite{ChPaTo26_3} for the partial regularity of the free boundary in higher dimensions. In this paper, we consider the special case with $K = 2$ populations in an annular domain in arbitrary dimensions. Using the uniqueness and the rotational invariance of the solution, we show that the free boundary consist of concentric spheres. Moreover, by an application of the free boundary condition derived in \cite{CL2}, we show that the free boundary is uniquely determined. We also examine how the radius of the free boundary change according to the domain, the interaction distance and the boundary data. In particular, we show that the free boundary converges to that of the adjacent model as the interaction distance tends to zero.
  We also study the one parameter family of the elliptic system, in which the domain, the interaction distance, and the boundary data depends on $t$. We derive an ODE for the radius of the free boundary in dimension $2$ and dimension $n \geq 3$ separately. Several examples are given and discussed.

</details>


### [111] [A Global Wavefront Set Condition for Defining Bony's Paraproduct Decomposition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02152)
*Josh Mott,Tim Van Hoose*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文利用全局波前集框架，建立了Besov分布乘法的Hörmander型条件之自然推广，并将其与Bony仿微分演算中的经典抛物积分解联系起来。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分布乘法是调和分析与偏微分方程中的核心问题，Hörmander条件给出了Sobolev框架下可乘的充分条件。Besov分布乘法的微局部分析尚缺乏与经典仿微分演算（Bony抛物积）相统一的视角，因此需要在全局波前集框架下推广该条件并建立两种方法的对应关系。

Method: 采用Coriasco、Johansson与Toft的全局波前集（global wavefront set）框架，针对Besov分布定义并建立Hörmander条件的自然推广；随后将该微局部分析条件与Bony仿微分演算中逐点乘法所对应的经典抛物积（paraproduct）分解进行对照与关联。

Result: 得到了Besov分布乘法的一个Hörmander型充分条件的自然推广，并揭示了全局波前集意义下的微局部分析刻画与抛物积分解之间的对应关系。

Conclusion: 该工作将分布乘法的微局部分析观点与经典仿微分演算统一起来，为在全局波前集框架下处理Besov分布乘法提供了理论工具与新的理解途径。

Abstract: We establish a natural generalization of the Hörmander condition for the multiplication of Besov distributions using the global wavefront set framework of Coriasco, Johansson, and Toft. Using this condition, we relate the microlocal perspective on multiplication of distributions to the classical paraproduct decomposition of pointwise multiplication arising in the paradifferential calculus of Bony.

</details>


<div id='math.SP'></div>

# math.SP [[Back]](#toc)

### [112] [Spherical density for real semisimple groups](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00298)
*Christopher Lutsko*

Main category: math.SP

TL;DR: 该论文证明：在连通半单实李群（有限中心）上，弱内射半径性质等价于同参数的球面密度假设（余紧阿基米德情形）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 球面密度假设与弱内射半径性质是谱几何与几何群论中的重要条件，二者关系此前仅有单向结果。作者希望在余紧Archimedean框架下建立等价刻画。

Method: 在余紧格的塔(Γ_N)上，利用谱壁附近的统一球面质量估计和一个正的前迹核进行论证；不依赖tempered谱估计，也不需要先验谱隙。

Result: 证明弱内射半径性质蕴含同参数的球面密度假设；结合Golubev-Kamber的逆定理，得到余紧Archimedean情形下二者的等价性。

Conclusion: 在余紧阿基米德设定中，弱内射半径性质与球面密度假设等价，为相关谱密度问题提供了完整的双向刻画。

Abstract: Let $G$ be a connected semisimple real Lie group with finite center and let $(Γ_N)$ be a tower of cocompact lattices. We prove that the weak injective radius property implies the spherical density hypothesis with the same parameter. Together with the converse theorem of Golubev and Kamber, this gives an equivalence in the cocompact Archimedean setting. The proof uses a uniform spherical-mass estimate near spectral walls and a positive pre-trace kernel, but no tempered-spectrum estimate or a priori spectral gap.

</details>


### [113] [Smooth perturbation theory and generic Cantor spectrum for multi-dimensional monotone quasiperiodic operators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00733)
*Ilya Kachkovskiy,Leonid Parnovski,Roman Shterenberg*

Main category: math.SP

TL;DR: 在扰动局域化区域中，研究具有单调锯齿型势的多维准周期Schrödinger算子，证明某给定谱隙闭合当且仅当（经由自然谱隙标记对应的）本征函数在原点消失；据此得到对一般采样函数谱为Cantor集且所有可能谱隙打开，并给出谱隙闭合的函数类及不同谱隙闭合难易的观察。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 准周期Schrödinger算子的谱结构（尤其是谱隙的打开/闭合，即Cantor谱结构）是该领域的核心问题。对多维、单调锯齿型势的情形，缺乏对谱隙闭合机制的精确刻画，本文旨在建立谱隙闭合与本征函数在原点取值之间的等价判据，并借此分析谱的Cantor性质与谱隙的普遍性。

Method: 考察扰动局域化区域内的多维准周期Schrödinger算子，势为单调锯齿型。引入自然谱隙标记，将每个谱隙与相应本征函数对应；证明谱隙闭合的充要条件是该本征函数在原点为零。利用该判据，对采样函数f进行一般性（generic）分析以判定谱隙打开情况，并构造使部分谱隙闭合的f的类别，同时比较不同谱隙的闭合难度。

Result: 建立了谱隙闭合的等价判据：给定谱隙闭合当且仅当对应本征函数在原点消失。由此证明对一般的采样函数f，算子谱为Cantor集且所有可能谱隙均打开；并给出存在谱隙闭合的一类采样函数f；进一步观察到某些谱隙比其他谱隙更难闭合。

Conclusion: 单调锯齿型势的多维准周期Schrödinger算子在扰动局域化区域内，其谱隙结构与原点处本征函数的消失行为精确对应；谱通常为全谱隙打开的Cantor集，但谱隙闭合可在特定采样函数类中发生，且不同谱隙的闭合难度存在差异。

Abstract: We consider multi-dimensional quasiperiodic Schrödinger operators with monotone sawtooth-type potentials in the perturbative localization regime. We prove that a given gap is closed iff a corresponding (via a natural gap labeling) eigenfunction vanishes at the origin. Using this, we show that for a generic choice of the sampling function $f$, the spectrum of such an operator is a Cantor set, with all possible gaps being open. We also provide a class of functions $f$ for which some gaps are closed. Finally, we observe that certain gaps are more difficult to close than the others.

</details>


### [114] [Inverse spectral results for the magnetic Schrödinger operator in low dimensions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01479)
*Devon Farrell,Anna Holloway,Nikhil Savale*

Main category: math.SP

TL;DR: 本文研究一维与二维半经典磁薛定谔算子，证明一维偶势与二维径向磁场分别可由相应算子的谱唯一确定，从而正面回答了Guillemin-Wang提出的猜想。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Guillemin-Wang此前的工作遗留了一个关于磁场谱确定性的猜想，本文旨在解决该问题，并推广其结论到一维偶势与二维径向磁场的情形。

Method: 在半经典框架下研究磁薛定谔算子：一维情形考察偶势对应的薛定谔算子，二维情形考察纯磁薛定谔算子下径向磁场的谱反演问题。

Result: 证明一维偶半经典势可由薛定谔算子谱确定；二维径向磁场可由纯磁薛定谔算子谱确定。

Conclusion: 两个谱确定结论成立，推广了Guillemin-Wang的先前结果并验证了其猜想。

Abstract: We consider the semiclassical magnetic Schrödinger operator in dimensions one and two. The first result is that an even one dimensional semiclassical potential is spectrally determined by the Schrödinger operator. The second result states that a two dimensional radial magnetic field is spectrally determined by a purely magnetic Schrödinger operator. This extends the earlier work of Guillemin-Wang and answers a conjecture of theirs therein.

</details>


### [115] [Eigenvalues of Hermitian Toeplitz matrices with Fisher--Hartwig symbols](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01498)
*Jacek Wszoła*

Main category: math.SP

TL;DR: 研究由对称Fisher-Hartwig符号生成Hermitian Toeplitz矩阵的特征值，利用离散Laplacian的Dirichlet-Neumann括号法给出非渐近显式界，证明所有特征值单重，并得到两阶渐近展开，覆盖α∈(0,2)全范围。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有结果仅覆盖α>1，α∈(0,1]区间存在空白；需要对该符号族在全参数范围内给出特征值的显式刻画与单重性证明。

Method: 对离散Laplacian使用Dirichlet-Neumann括号（bracketing）技术，并构造此前未在该情境下使用的近似特征向量。

Result: 建立个体特征值的显式非渐近界；证明所有特征值单重；当矩阵规模趋于无穷时，对每个特征值给出带显式余项界的两项近似。

Conclusion: 方法填补了α∈(0,2)全范围的空白，统一并推广了已知仅适用于α>1的结果。

Abstract: We investigate the eigenvalues of Hermitian Toeplitz matrices generated by the symmetric Fisher-Hartwig symbol $\hat a(z) = (1-z)^{α/2}(1-z^{-1})^{α/2}$ for $α\in (0,2)$. Using Dirichlet-Neumann bracketing of the discrete Laplacian, we establish explicit, non-asymptotic bounds for the individual eigenvalues. As a consequence, we prove that all eigenvalues are simple. We also obtain a two-term approximation for every eigenvalue, with explicit bounds on the remainder, as the size of the matrix tends to infinity. While known results are limited to $α> 1$, we bridge this gap by covering the full range of $α\in (0,2)$. Our approach uses a construction of approximate eigenvectors that has not previously been applied in this setting.

</details>


<div id='math.DG'></div>

# math.DG [[Back]](#toc)

### [116] [Detecting Einstein metrics on products of spheres](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00066)
*Anna Siffert*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 在 \(S^5\times S^2\)、\(S^5\times S^3\)、\(S^5\times S^4\)、\(S^7\times S^2\) 上构造无穷多个两两非等距的实解析 Einstein 度量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 Einstein 度量（Riemannian 乘积、经典 Böhm 两和式度量等）无法覆盖这些流形上的全部几何；作者希望在 cohomogeneity-one 框架下产生大量全新的、非乘积非经典的 Einstein 度量，以丰富这些流形的 Einstein 模空间。

Method: 采用 \(dt^2+a(t)^2g_{S^p}+b(t)^2g_{S^p}+c(t)^2g_{S^q}\)（\(p,q\ge2,\ 2p+q\le8\)）的 cohomogeneity-one 度量形式。从某一奇异轨道处两个非塌缩半径极小的解出发，对该区域放大后 Einstein 方程趋于 Ricci-flat 问题，其解以衰减振荡方式趋向锥。不追踪完整且振荡加剧的 shooting 映射，而是从 Ricci-flat 极限中提取两个响应量：一个确定公共振荡相位，去掉该相位后第二个闭合条件归结为标量变号；再用一致的内外匹配把极限变号传递到精确 Einstein 方程。

Result: 在四个流形上得到无穷多个两两非等距的实解析 Einstein 度量；除有限个成员外，均不同构也不同缩于 Riemannian 乘积或经典两和式 Böhm 度量。随初始尺度趋于零，相位可任意多次旋转，闭合条件被无穷多次满足。

Conclusion: 通过 Ricci-flat 极限的相位—标量变号机制与内外匹配，可在给定流形上无穷次满足闭合条件，从而产生无穷多非等距、非乘积、非经典的两和式 Einstein 度量。

Abstract: On \[ S^5\times S^2,\qquad S^5\times S^3,\qquad S^5\times S^4,\qquad S^7\times S^2, \] we construct infinitely many pairwise non-isometric real-analytic Einstein metrics. After discarding finitely many members, none is isometric or homothetic to a Riemannian product or to a classical two-summand Böhm metric. The metrics have the form \[ dt^2+a(t)^2g_{S^p}+b(t)^2g_{S^p}+c(t)^2g_{S^q}, \qquad p,q\ge2,\qquad 2p+q\le8. \]
  The construction starts with solutions for which the two non-collapsing radii at one singular orbit are very small. After magnifying this region, the Einstein equations approach a Ricci-flat problem whose solutions tend to a cone by a decaying oscillation. The main point is to determine exactly which part of this oscillation can still be seen by the closing conditions at the other end of the manifold. Rather than following the full, increasingly oscillatory shooting map, we extract two response quantities from the Ricci-flat limit. One determines the common oscillatory phase; after that phase has been removed, the second closing condition is reduced to a scalar sign change. A uniform inner--outer matching argument transfers this limiting sign change to the exact Einstein equations. As the initial scale tends to zero, the phase makes arbitrarily many turns, and the closing conditions are therefore met infinitely many times.

</details>


### [117] [Gradient Ricci Solitons on PTR-Submanifolds of Complex Space Forms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00077)
*Jae Won Lee*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 研究复空间形式中部分全实(PTR)子流形上的梯度Ricci孤立子，建立Gauss-孤立子方程对切向Kähler算子平方的重构，并给出谱编码、λ上界、刚性及不存在性等结果，附平坦多级Gaussian模型与非平坦Segre CR-乘积作为一致性检验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在复空间形式中研究梯度Ricci孤立子与PTR子流形的交互，旨在将PTR切向谱这一外在/内在混合对象用内在孤立子数据与第二基本形式表达，从而统一刻画曲率、孤立子结构与子流形几何之间的约束关系。

Method: 在非零环境全纯截面曲率下推导Gauss-孤立子方程，用Hessian自同态与一个外在Ricci算子重构切向Kähler算子平方P²；结合第二基本形式分析PTR切向谱的编码方式；对紧致极小PTR孤立子施加正环境曲率及相对零化条件；证明无迹各向异性恒等式、平行数据刚性判据与紧致实超曲面不存在性定理；构造平坦多级Gaussian模型与Segre CR-乘积作显式验证。

Result: 获得切向谱由内在孤立子数据与第二基本形式编码的重构公式；对正曲率下紧致极小PTR孤立子证明λ≤c(n-1)/4，并在相对零化条件下得到严格不等式；得到紧致平凡性判据、精确无迹各向异性恒等式、平行数据刚性结果及紧致实超曲面的不存在性定理；两类显式模型确证重构公式并识别其极限情形。

Conclusion: 梯度Ricci孤立子与PTR子流形结构的耦合可由Gauss-孤立子重构框架统一处理，切向谱完全由内在孤立子数据与外在第二基本形式决定，且曲率符号对紧致情形施加了尖锐的谱与存在性限制。

Abstract: We study gradient Ricci solitons on partially totally real (PTR) submanifolds of complex space forms. In nonzero ambient holomorphic sectional curvature, the Gauss--soliton equation reconstructs the square $P^2$ of the tangential Kähler operator from the Hessian endomorphism and an extrinsic Ricci operator. Consequently, the PTR tangency spectrum is encoded by intrinsic soliton data together with the second fundamental form. For compact minimal PTR solitons with positive ambient curvature we obtain $λ\le c(n-1)/4$, with a strict version under a relative-nullity condition. We also prove compact triviality criteria, an exact trace-free anisotropy identity, a parallel-data rigidity result, and a nonexistence theorem for compact real hypersurfaces. A flat multi-level Gaussian model and a nonflat Segre $CR$-product provide explicit consistency checks and identify the limiting cases of the reconstruction formulas.

</details>


### [118] [A Quantitative Reeb-Form Obstruction for Real Hypersurfaces of Strict Nearly Kähler Six-Manifolds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00078)
*Jae Won Lee,Chul Woo Lee*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 在六维严格近Kähler流形的定向实超曲面$M^5$上，诱导Reeb形式$\eta$满足处处非退化：$(d\eta)_p\neq0$对所有$p\in M$成立；由此Yano半对称度量联络的Ricci张量处处非对称。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究六维严格近Kähler流形中超曲面的诱导近切触结构，澄清其Reeb形式是否可能退化（即$d\eta=0$）这一基本问题，并给出了由超曲面恒等式与六维严格近Kähler代数直接导出的障碍性结论。

Method: 采用局部论证：仅使用超曲面恒等式$\nabla_X\xi=\varphi AX-G(X,N)$及六维严格近Kähler代数。假设$(d\eta)_p=0$，导出$\varphi A+A\varphi=2G_N$，进而迫使Hopf条件；在切触超平面$\mathcal D_p=\ker\eta_p$上分析$\varphi A+A\varphi$与$\varphi$交换、而$G_N$与$\varphi$反交换且$G_N^2=-\alpha I_{\mathcal D_p}\,(\alpha>0)$的矛盾。并考察Yano半对称度量联络$\widehat\nabla_XY=\nabla_XY+\eta(Y)X-g(X,Y)\xi$的曲率。

Result: 证明了点态障碍$(d\eta)_p\neq0$（对所有$p\in M$）。作为联络论推论，在五维情形有$\operatorname{Skew}\operatorname{Ric}^{\widehat\nabla}=-\tfrac32\,d\eta$，从而$\operatorname{Ric}^{\widehat\nabla}$处处非对称。该障碍同时排除了任何包含$d\eta=0$条件的局部诱导几乎切触类。结论在标准齐性严格近Kähler空间上得到说明。

Conclusion: 六维严格近Kähler流形的实超曲面天然携带非退化的诱导Reeb形式，其诱导几乎切触结构不可能是切触（$d\eta=0$）类型；这一几何障碍并体现为Yano半对称度量联络Ricci张量的处处非对称性。

Abstract: Let $M^5$ be an oriented real hypersurface of a six-dimensional strict nearly Kähler manifold $(\widetilde M^6,\widetilde g,J)$, and let $ξ=-JN$ and $η=g(\,\cdot\,,ξ)$ denote the induced Reeb field and Reeb one-form. We prove the pointwise obstruction \[ (dη)_p\neq0\qquad\text{for every }p\in M. \] The proof is local and uses only the hypersurface identity \[ \nabla_Xξ=φAX-G(X,N) \] together with the six-dimensional strict nearly Kähler algebra. If $(dη)_p=0$, then \[ φA+Aφ=2G_N,\qquad G_NX=G(X,N), \] which forces the Hopf condition. On the contact hyperplane $\mathcal D_p=\kerη_p$, the endomorphism $φA+Aφ$ then commutes with $φ$, whereas $G_N$ anti-commutes with $φ$ and satisfies \[ G_N^2=-αI_{\mathcal D_p},\qquad α>0. \] This contradiction proves the obstruction. As a connection-theoretic consequence, for the standard Yano semi-symmetric metric connection associated with $ξ$, \[ \widehat\nabla_XY=\nabla_XY+η(Y)X-g(X,Y)ξ, \] one has in dimension five \[ \operatorname{Skew}\operatorname{Ric}^{\widehat\nabla}=-\frac32\,dη, \] and hence $\operatorname{Ric}^{\widehat\nabla}$ is nowhere symmetric. The obstruction also rules out any local induced almost contact class whose defining conditions include $dη=0$. We illustrate the result on the standard homogeneous strict nearly Kähler spaces.

</details>


### [119] [A counterexample to Yau's bounded-mean-curvature embedding question](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00153)
*Haoxuan Cheng*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 该文否定了Yau的一个问题：构造出R^4上完备、Ricci曲率有界、单射半径正、但全曲率无界的度量，从而任何到有限维欧氏空间的C^2等距浸入必有平均曲率无界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Yau曾问：完备、Ricci曲率有界且单射半径正的Riemannian流形，是否总能等距嵌入某欧氏空间并具有有界平均曲率。该问题涉及曲率有界性、嵌入理论与平均曲率之间的相互关系，本文旨在给出反例以否定这一猜测。

Method: 在R^4上构造光滑完备度量：度量由互不相交的“曲率尖峰”拼接而成，使得Ricci缩并保持一致有界，而全曲率无界；利用短测地曲率估计与Jacobi方程证明单射半径的一致正下界；再用Gauss方程说明任何C^2等距浸入有限维欧氏空间时平均曲率必然无界。该构造可推广至任意维数≥4。

Result: 得到一个反例：R^4上存在完备光滑度量，Ricci曲率有界、单射半径为正、但全曲率无界；因此其到有限维欧氏空间的任何C^2等距浸入均不可能具有有界平均曲率。构造对任意维数≥4成立；在四维时还可使Ricci曲率趋于零。

Conclusion: Yau关于有界Ricci曲率与正单射半径蕴含有界平均曲率等距嵌入的猜测不成立，反例表明需额外假设（如全曲率有界）才能保证有界平均曲率嵌入。

Abstract: Yau asked whether every complete Riemannian manifold with bounded Ricci curvature and positive injectivity radius admits an isometric embedding into some Euclidean space with bounded mean curvature. We construct a counterexample: a smooth complete metric on $\mathbb{R}^4$ with bounded Ricci curvature, positive injectivity radius, and unbounded full curvature. The Gauss equation then forces every $C^2$ isometric immersion of this metric into a finite-dimensional Euclidean space to have unbounded mean curvature. The metric is assembled from disjoint curvature peaks whose Ricci contractions stay uniformly bounded. A short-geodesic curvature estimate and the Jacobi equation give the uniform injectivity-radius bound. The construction works in every dimension at least four, and in dimension four the Ricci curvature may be chosen to tend to zero at infinity.

</details>


### [120] [Minimality and Nonlinear Spectral Rigidity of Hypersurfaces in Real Space Forms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00214)
*Zihao Wang*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 该文研究欧氏、球面与双曲空间中超曲面的非线性平均曲率谱方程，在可容许解析响应F与外围截面曲率c下给出谱参数的极小性阈值（σ≥nc）与σ<nc时的梯度点平均曲率界的严格结果，统一处理三类空间形式，并证明Chen猜想（欧氏余维一）及负常曲率下的双调和极小性，同时给出脐带模型说明阈值最优与若干紧致模型的定量局部刚性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究者希望统一并推广非线性平均曲率型（谱）方程在各空间形式中的极小性与刚性理论，特别是解决长期悬而未决的Chen猜想（欧氏余维一）以及负常曲率中的双调和极小性问题，同时澄清谱参数与外围曲率之间的临界阈值。

Method: 对可容许解析响应F引入谱参数σ并与外围截面曲率c比较；利用跨三种几何通用的实解析延拓与焦痕（focal-trace）论证；考虑F(t)=t^{p-1}（p>1）及满足显式宽度条件的正混合类；构造显式脐带模型；并用定量论证证明模环境等距意义下的局部刚性。

Result: 证明当σ≥nc时解为极小，当σ<nc时在梯度点获得严格平均曲率界；结果涵盖所有幂型响应及满足宽度条件的正混合；特别地证明了欧氏余维一的Chen猜想与负常曲率下的双调和极小性，且无需完备性或全纯（holonomicity）假设；显式脐带模型表明无条件谱极小范围是尖锐的；并证明了三维空间中紧致非极小测地球面及球面中等半径Clifford超曲面的定量局部刚性（模环境等距），适用于模型振幅附近F,F'>0的光滑法则。

Conclusion: 该工作以统一的解析延拓与焦痕方法刻画了非线性平均曲率谱方程在三种空间形式中的极小性阈值与严格估计，解决了Chen猜想（欧氏余维一）和负常曲率双调和极小性等关键问题，并通过脐带模型确认阈值最优；同时建立了若干典型紧致模型的定量局部刚性。

Abstract: We study nonlinear mean-curvature spectral equations for hypersurfaces in Euclidean, spherical and hyperbolic space. For an admissible analytic response $F$ and ambient sectional curvature $c$, we prove minimality when the spectral parameter satisfies $σ\ge nc$, and strict mean-curvature bounds at gradient points when $σ<nc$. The class includes every power $F(t)=t^{p-1}$, $p>1$, and positive mixtures satisfying an explicit width condition. In particular, the result proves Chen's conjecture in Euclidean codimension one and biharmonic minimality in constant negative curvature, without completeness or holonomicity. A common real-analytic continuation and focal-trace argument treats all three geometries. Explicit umbilical models show that the unconditional spectral minimality range is sharp. Independently, we prove quantitative local rigidity, modulo ambient isometries, of compact nonminimal geodesic spheres in all three geometries and proper equal-radius Clifford hypersurfaces in spheres, for smooth laws with $F,F'>0$ near the model amplitude.

</details>


### [121] [Minimal equatorial fillings of low index](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00247)
*Jacob Bernstein,Daniel Ketover*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文研究 $\mathbb{S}^3$ 中具有最低可能 Morse 指标的极小赤道填充曲面，并获得其几何与拓扑限制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 极小曲面（极小子流形）的 Morse 指标刻画其稳定性，最低指标情形往往是分类问题的关键切入点；赤道填充作为 $\mathbb{S}^3$ 中边界位于赤道大圆上的极小曲面，其低指标情形尚缺乏系统的几何与拓扑限制。

Method: 利用极小曲面/Morse 指标理论，对 $\mathbb{S}^3$ 中以赤道 $\mathbb{S}^1$ 为边界的极小填充曲面，在最可能的低 Morse 指标条件下进行几何与拓扑分析。

Result: 得到最低可能 Morse 指标的极小赤道填充曲面在几何（如对称性、曲率性质）与拓扑（如亏格、连通性）方面的限制条件。

Conclusion: 最低指标的极小赤道填充受到强几何与拓扑约束，表明此类低指标极小子流形结构高度受限。

Abstract: We obtain restrictions on the geometry and topology of minimal equatorial fillings in $\mathbb{S}^3$ of the lowest possible Morse index.

</details>


### [122] [An exact hypergeometric Weierstrass representation for Smyth's free-boundary minimal disk in the regular tetrahedron](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00434)
*Steven Finch*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文给出正四面体中Smyth所证明的三片嵌入极小圆盘的显式Weierstrass数据，Gauss映射为角(π/4, π/2, πβ/2)的圆孤三角Schwarz函数，无附加参数与自由参数，并给出κ的Γ值闭式、g′的Wronskian闭式、总曲率2π(2β−1)及数据不可代数的刚性结论，数值结果与2013年Surface Evolver计算吻合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Smyth(1984)仅以存在性方式证明了任意四面体内存在三片与边界正交的嵌入极小圆盘，其构造依赖于斜四边形Plateau解的共轭曲面，但未给出显式解析数据；本文旨在对正四面体这一对称性最高的特例显式求解Weierstrass表示，并揭示其代数/超越性质与刚性，同时验证长期存在的数值结果。因此动机是填补显式构造与可计算性空白，并厘清该极小曲面的解析与数论性质。

Method: 取Smyth的斜四边形Q，其边为c_iν_i（c_i为面φ_i面积，ν_i为外法向）；对正四面体显式求解相应Plateau问题的共轭极小曲面。利用对称性将Gauss映射识别为圆孤三角形（角π/4, π/2, πβ/2，β=arccos(−1/3)/π）的Schwarz三角函数，超几何参数关于β线性。通过单值性(arguments)分析、Wronskian恒等式与Γ函数闭式确定κ和g′，并借助对称平方(symmetric-square)结构讨论数据的刚性；最后以高精度数值延拓（28位）与Surface Evolver结果对比。

Result: 得到正四面体情形的显式Weierstrass数据：Gauss映射为上述圆孤三角形Schwarz函数，超几何参数线性于β，且无附加参数、无自由参数；给出κ的Γ值闭式与g′的Wronskian闭式；总曲率为2π(2β−1)；揭示数据的对称平方结构及刚性：因β/2无理，射影单值群稠密，故数据不可为代数。数值上将表示延拓至28位，Area/ℓ²=0.2172341554075574483760009126…，与Ken Brakke 2013年Surface Evolver计算一致。

Conclusion: 对正四面体，Smyth的存在性极小圆盘可由无自由参数的显式超几何/Schwarz三角数据完全描述，其Gauss映射、度量与总曲率均具闭式，且因β/2无理而本质超越、不可代数化；高精度数值验证了既有Surface Evolver结果，并给出该特例的刚性刻画。

Abstract: Smyth (1984) proved that every tetrahedron $T\subset\mathbb{R}^3$ contains three embedded minimal disks meeting $\partial T$ orthogonally, obtained as conjugate surfaces of Plateau solutions in a skew quadrilateral $Q$ whose edges are $c_iν_i$, where $\varphi_1,\varphi_2,\varphi_3,\varphi_4$ are faces of $T$, $c_i=\operatorname{Area}(\varphi_i)$ and $ν_i$ are outward normal vectors. For the $\textit{regular}$ tetrahedron we give the Weierstrass data explicitly. The Gauss map is a Schwarz triangle function for a circular-arc triangle with angles $(π/4,\ π/2,\ πβ/2)$, where $β=\arccos(-1/3)/π$; the hypergeometric parameters are linear in $β$ and there is $\textit{no accessory parameter and no free parameter}$. We record a closed form for $κ$ in $Γ$-values, a Wronskian closed form for $g'$, the exact total curvature $2π(2β-1)$, the symmetric-square structure of the data, and a rigidity statement: since $β/2$ is irrational, the projective monodromy is dense and the data $\textit{cannot}$ be algebraic. Numerically the representation extends to 28 digits -- $\operatorname{Area}/\ell^{2}=0.2172341554075574483760009126\ldots$ -- confirming a 2013 Surface Evolver computation of Ken Brakke.

</details>


### [123] [Strict Log-Concavity of First Dirichlet Eigenfunctions under Symmetry in $\mathbb{CP}^n$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00716)
*Jia-Lin Hsu*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 证明了在 $\mathbb{CP}^n$（$n=2,3,4$）中，边界凸的光滑区域上、关于标准 $U(1)\times U(n-1)$ 作用不变的第一 Dirichlet 正特征函数是严格对数凹的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对数凹性（log-concavity）是特征函数几何性质的核心问题，在欧氏空间已有经典结果（如 Brascamp–Lieb 理论）。在复射影空间等正曲率流形上，凸性概念与欧氏情形差异显著，相应的对数凹性结论此前并不清楚，本文旨在将这一研究推广到 $\mathbb{CP}^n$ 的对称情形。

Method: 在 $\mathbb{CP}^n$ 中考虑边界凸（convex boundary）的光滑区域，取关于标准 $U(1)\times U(n-1)$ 作用不变的第一 Dirichlet 特征函数；利用该对称性将问题约化，并结合复几何中凸性（如 Levi 凸、测地凸）与对数凹性的分析工具进行证明。

Result: 在所设条件下，对 $n=2,3,4$，证明了该正第一 Dirichlet 特征函数严格对数凹（strictly log-concave）。

Conclusion: 结果给出了 $\mathbb{CP}^n$ 在低维（$n\le 4$）及对称性约束下第一特征函数对数凹性的肯定答案，为高维及无对称性情形留下了开放问题。

Abstract: We prove that the positive first Dirichlet eigenfunction of every smooth domain with convex boundary in $\mathbb{CP}^n$ invariant under the standard $U(1)\times U(n-1)$-action is strictly log-concave for $n=2,3,4$.

</details>


### [124] [The Wasserstein space of the circle is not flat](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00625)
*André Magalhães de Sá Gomes,Christian S. Rodrigues,Luiz A. B. San Martin*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文证明了圆周的Wasserstein空间不平凡地具有非零截面曲率，纠正了"底流形一维则Wasserstein空间平坦"的普遍预期，并在Lott全局标架下以闭式给出Otto度量与Levi-Civita联络系数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实数轴与区间的Wasserstein空间是平坦的，这使得人们普遍预期底流形为一维时Wasserstein空间也平坦。本文旨在检验该预期，研究圆周这一紧致一维流形上W_2空间的几何（曲率）性质。

Method: 在由拉普拉斯算子特征函数诱导的P^∞(M)切丛全局标架（Lott观察到的）下进行Riemann几何计算：证明该标架是每个切空间的Schauder基，写出Otto度量及其Levi-Civita联络的表达式；在圆周上得到P^∞(S^1)度量与联络系数的闭式（密度的Fourier系数有限组合），并通过辛形式刻画截面曲率，同时直接从W_2定义验证。

Result: 圆周的Wasserstein空间不平坦；其截面曲率由Fourier系数上的辛形式支配，平面平坦当且仅当它关于该形式是迷向的；在第n个Fourier模态张成的平面上曲率上界为3n^2，等号仅在均匀测度处取到，故曲率无界。结果同时表明P_2(S^1)中均匀测度的任何邻域都不是CAT(0)。此外，将扩张圆周映射在其不变测度处推前作用的导数解读为Fourier频率上的加权移位。

Conclusion: 圆周Wasserstein空间具有无界的正曲率，纠正了"一维底流形导致Wasserstein空间平坦"的既有预期；Lott全局标架为P^∞(M)提供了Schauder基与闭式几何系数，成为分析Otto度量、联络及动力学推前映射的有力工具。

Abstract: We show that the Wasserstein space of the circle is not flat, in contrast with the flatness of the Wasserstein spaces of the real line and of an interval. Its sectional curvature is governed by a symplectic form on the Fourier coefficients, so that a plane is flat exactly when it is isotropic for that form, and on the plane spanned by the $n$-th Fourier modes the curvature is bounded above by $3n^2$, with equality exactly at the uniform measure. It is therefore unbounded. We compute it inside the formal Riemannian structure of Otto and confirm it directly from the definition of $W_2$, which also shows that no neighbourhood of the uniform measure in $P_2(S^1)$ is CAT(0). The result corrects the widely stated expectation that a Wasserstein space is flat whenever its base manifold is one-dimensional.
  The computation is carried out in the global frame of the tangent bundle of $P^\infty(M)$ induced by the eigenfunctions of the Laplacian of $M$, whose existence was observed by Lott. We prove that it is a Schauder basis of every tangent space and write Otto's metric and its Levi-Civita connection in it. On the circle this gives the metric and connection coefficients of $P^\infty(S^1)$ in closed form, as finite combinations of the Fourier coefficients of the density. A final section puts the frame to work on a computation from dynamics, reading the derivative of the push-forward action of an expanding circle map at its invariant measure as a weighted shift on the Fourier frequencies.

</details>


### [125] [The Heat Kernel of the Hodge Laplacian on an Invariant Subspace of 1-forms on the Hyperbolic Space $\mathbb{H}^{n}$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00653)
*Petros Siasos*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文给出了实双曲空间 $\mathbb{H}^n$ 上 $1$-形式不变子空间内 Hodge Laplacian 热核的显式公式，建立了跨维度的 Millson 型递推关系，并据此得到尖锐的一致估计与长时间渐近行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典标量热核的界已有完善理论，但实双曲空间上 $1$-形式（微分形式）热核的显式刻画与定量估计尚不完整，本文旨在将标量结果推广到 $1$-形式上。

Method: 利用 warped product 分解将问题化归为特定子空间上的分量算子，分别对奇数维与偶数维情形求出闭式表达式；并建立连接不同维度热核的 Millson 型递推关系。

Result: 获得 $\mathbb{H}^n$ 上 $1$-形式 Hodge 热核的精确闭式公式（奇、偶维均适用），以及由这些显式表示导出的尖锐一致估计和长时间渐近行为。

Conclusion: 该工作把经典标量热核界成功推广到实双曲空间上的 $1$-形式，为微分形式热核的定量分析提供了显式工具与递推框架。

Abstract: In this paper, we construct explicit formulas for the heat kernel of the Hodge Laplacian restricted to an invariant subspace of $1$-forms on the real hyperbolic space $\mathbb{H}^{n}$. Using warped product decompositions, the problem is reduced to component operators on specific subspaces, yielding precise closed-form expressions for both odd and even dimensions. We further establish Millson-type recurrence relations that connect heat kernels across differing dimensions. Based on these explicit representations, we derive sharp uniform estimates and long-time asymptotic behavior, extending classical scalar heat kernel bounds to $1$-forms on real hyperbolic spaces.

</details>


### [126] [Algebraic limits of Hermitian-Yang-Mills connections on Kähler surfaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01221)
*Keshu Zhou*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 该文为Kähler曲面上Hermitian-Yang-Mills联络建立统一几何化的Hörmander L²估计，将光滑HYM联络的Uhlenbeck收敛实现为解析平坦族内的代数收敛，并给出可光滑化族的连续性定理与固定拓扑μ-半稳定层的boundedness结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Kähler曲面上HYM联络的模空间研究需要统一的L²估计工具，并需理解Uhlenbeck收敛（解析范畴）与代数收敛（代数几何范畴）之间的关系，以便把解析侧的分析结果转化为代数几何中的连续性、boundedness等结论。

Method: 构造几何化的方式，对具界能量的Hermitian-Yang-Mills联络统一实施Hörmander L²估计；在此基础上证明Kähler曲面上光滑HYM联络的Uhlenbeck收敛可在解析平坦族内实现为代数收敛。

Result: 建立了HYM联络的统一Hörmander L²估计；实现了Uhlenbeck收敛作为解析平坦族中的代数收敛；作为应用，证明了可光滑化族的连续性定理，以及固定拓扑下μ-半稳定层的boundedness结果。

Conclusion: 该几何化框架将解析收敛与代数收敛联系起来，为HYM联络的模问题提供了新的工具与思路。

Abstract: We develop a geometric way to uniformly carry out Hörmander's $L^2$-estimates for Hermitian-Yang-Mills connections with bounded energy on Kähler surfaces. We then show the Uhlenbeck convergence of smooth HYM connections on Kähler surfaces can be realized as algebraic convergence inside an analytic flat family. As applications, we prove a continuity theorem for smoothable families and a boundedness result for $μ$-semistable sheaves with fixed topology. We also discuss potential applications for the moduli problem.

</details>


### [127] [Riemannian Structures on Quotients in Elastic Diffeology via Q-Charts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01346)
*Yusuke Shiobara*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文研究Q-charts（Blohmann弹性可沿其下降的diffeological空间子duction），证明弹性diffeology的黎曼结构（垂直/水平/全联络、度量、Levi-Civita联络、测地线）沿Q-charts下降，并应用于无理环面的黎曼几何。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在diffeological空间范畴中，弹性diffeology提供了黎曼几何的框架，但如何沿subduction下降黎曼结构（尤其是通过拟群不变性刻画）此前缺乏系统结果；同时无理环面等商空间的黎曼几何需要完整刻画。

Method: 沿Q-charts $φ\colon X\to Y$ 建立下降理论：证明迭代切丛 $T^nX$ 及其纤维积 $T_kX$ 是 $φ$ 下对应丛的拉回，利用纤维保持过渡拟群 $Ψ(φ)$ 的不变性给出结构间的双射，并验证无挠性、有效性、平坦性、满性、Levi-Civita性质与测地线方程的双向保持。对diffeologically discrete群主作用的商，全局提升测地线，证明$\R$为curve object的弹性空间类在商下封闭，完整测地流下降。

Result: 建立黎曼结构沿Q-charts的双向对应与性质保持定理；对离散群主作用商实现测地线全局提升与完整流下降；完全确定无理环面 $\Tor_α=\R/(\Z+α\Z)$ 的黎曼几何：每个黎曼度量是 $\vartheta^2$（$dt$ 诱导的一形式）的正常数倍，垂直联络构成单参数平坦有效联络族，其中唯一成员为所有度量的Levi-Civita联络，该联络测地流完整但诱导伪距离恒为零。

Conclusion: 弹性diffeology的黎曼装置可沿Q-charts良好下降，拟群不变性给出结构的双射刻画；无理环面的黎曼几何表现奇异——唯一Levi-Civita联络虽测地完整却使伪距离恒为零，说明完整测地流与度量性可分离。

Abstract: Following Miyamoto, we consider Q-charts, a class of subductions $φ\colon X\to Y$ of diffeological spaces along which Blohmann's elasticity descends. We show that the Riemannian apparatus of elastic diffeology---vertical, horizontal and full connections, Riemannian metrics, Levi-Civita connections and geodesics---also descends along Q-charts. More precisely, structures on $Y$ correspond bijectively to structures on $X$ that are invariant under the pseudogroup $Ψ(φ)$ of fibre-preserving transitions, and torsion-freeness, effectivity, flatness, fullness, the Levi-Civita property and the geodesic equation are preserved in both directions. The key input is that the iterated tangent bundles $T^nX$ and their fibre products $T_kX$ are the pullbacks along $φ$ of the corresponding bundles over $Y$.
  For quotients by principal actions of diffeologically discrete groups, we lift geodesics globally, prove that the class of elastic spaces on which $\R$ is a curve object is closed under such quotients, and show that complete geodesic flows descend. As an application, we determine the Riemannian geometry of the irrational tori $\Tor_α=\R/(\Z+α\Z)$ completely. Let $\vartheta$ be the one-form on $\Tor_α$ induced by $dt$ on $\R$. Every Riemannian metric is a positive constant multiple of $\vartheta^2$, and the vertical connections form a one-parameter family of flat effective connections, a single member of which is the unique Levi-Civita connection of every metric. This connection has a complete geodesic flow, yet the induced pseudo-distance vanishes identically.

</details>


### [128] [Rigidity of volume-minimizing free boundary hypersurfaces in high dimensional Riemannian manifolds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01392)
*Sanghun Lee*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文建立了体积估计与刚性结果，针对$(n+1)$维黎曼流形$M$中体积最小化的自由边界超曲面。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究自由边界极小/稳定超曲面的体积界与等号情形下的流形刚性，是极小曲面理论与黎曼几何中的核心问题，本文旨在给出依赖标量曲率与边界第二基本形式的定量刻画。

Method: 利用稳定性（stable）假设与自由边界变分公式，结合标量曲率及$\partial M$的第二基本形式，建立稳定极小自由边界超曲面的体积估计；并通过分析等号成立条件推导$M$的刚性。

Result: 给出稳定极小自由边界超曲面体积由$M$的标量曲率和$\partial M$第二基本形式控制的上界；证明了相应等号情形下$M$的刚性结果。

Conclusion: 体积估计与刚性结果共同刻画了等号情形下流形的结构，为自由边界极小曲面问题提供了新的定量与刚性刻画。

Abstract: In this paper, we establish a volume estimate and an associated rigidity result for volume-minimizing free boundary hypersurfaces in an $(n+1)$-dimensional Riemannian manifold $M$. First, we derive a volume estimate for stable minimal free boundary hypersurfaces in terms of the scalar curvature of $M$ and the second fundamental form of the boundary $\partial M$. We then prove rigidity results of $M$ in the corresponding equality cases.

</details>


### [129] [The sphere is the only closed surface satisfying the fixed-width Archimedean property](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01550)
*Mijia Lai*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文证明：在$\mathbb R^3$中的连通光滑闭嵌入曲面中，对任一固定宽度，满足“被两平行平面截出的条带面积为$2\pi h$”这一性质的曲面只能是单位球面。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 阿基米德的经典结论是单位球面具有等面积条带性质（面积为$2\pi h$），作者希望研究该性质能否反向刻画单位球面，即该性质是否唯一确定球面。

Method: 在连通光滑闭嵌入曲面的范围内，考察任意固定宽度$h$（两平行平面间距为$h$且均与曲面相交）所截出的区域面积等于$2\pi h$的条件，并据此进行刻画。

Result: 证明了对于单个固定宽度，该等面积条带性质在连通光滑闭嵌入曲面中唯一刻画单位球面。

Conclusion: 阿基米德的等面积条带性质是一个刚性刻画条件：只需一个固定宽度即可确定单位球面。

Abstract: Among Archimedes' many celebrated discoveries is a striking fact: the region of the unit sphere between two parallel planes, both meeting the sphere and separated by a distance $h$, has area $2πh$. We prove that, among connected smooth closed embedded surfaces in $\mathbb R^3$, this property characterizes the unit sphere for a single fixed width.

</details>


### [130] [Solvable Algebraic Generalized Ricci Solitons in low dimensions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01608)
*Lorenzo Mucciante*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 该文研究可解李代数上的代数广义Ricci孤立子，证明了非平凡解必为膨胀型，给出阿贝尔李代数一维扩张上存在性的必要条件，并分类了三维李代数及四维可解幺模李代数上的此类孤立子（按等距与缩放）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 广义Ricci孤立子是Ricci孤立子的推广，与Ricci流、Einstein度量等几何结构密切相关。作者希望将相关结构理论从黎曼情形推广到可解李代数（即代数层面）框架，并补充低维分类这一基础性空缺。

Method: 采用代数化方法，将广义Ricci孤立子方程置于可解李代数上，通过结构分析（子代数、导代数、一维扩张构造）推导必要性条件；随后对三维李代数和四维可解幺模李代数逐一求解方程并作等距与缩放意义下的分类。

Result: 证明所有非平凡可解代数广义Ricci孤立子必为膨胀型（expanding）；给出阿贝尔李代数一维扩张上存在此类孤立子的必要条件；完成三维李代数与四维可解幺模李代数上代数广义Ricci孤立子的分类（等距与缩放意义下）。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了可解李代数上代数广义Ricci孤立子的结构理论与低维分类，为后续更高维分类及与Ricci流、Einstein型度量的联系提供基础。

Abstract: We prove new structural results for algebraic generalized Ricci solitons on solvable Lie algebras and we discuss the existence of examples in low dimensions. We establish that all non-trivial solvable algebraic generalized Ricci solitons must be expanding and give necessary conditions for the existence of such solitons on one-dimensional extensions of abelian Lie algebras. Finally, we classify algebraic generalized Ricci solitons on three-dimensional Lie algebras, and algebraic generalized Ricci solitons on four-dimensional solvable unimodular Lie algebras, up to isometry and scaling.

</details>


### [131] [Finite Time Singularities of the Kähler Ricci Flow on Compact Kähler Surfaces are of Type I](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01643)
*Yeyun Xu,Linfeng Zhou*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 在紧Kähler曲面上，对有限时间体积坍缩的Kähler–Ricci流建立了Type I曲率界，无需对称性假设或初始Kähler类有理的假定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 体积坍缩的Kähler–Ricci流的奇点分析通常依赖对称性假设或初始Kähler类有理的条件，本文旨在去除这些限制，在更一般的紧Kähler曲面上研究有限时间坍缩的曲率行为。

Method: 结合局部辛拓扑方法与梯度Kähler–Ricci收缩孤立子的分类，并利用Bamler的紧性与结构理论获得所需收缩极限。

Result: 证明了紧Kähler曲面上有限时间体积坍缩Kähler–Ricci流的Type I曲率界，且不施加对称性假设、初始Kähler类无需有理。

Conclusion: 该结果为无对称性、非有理Kähler类情形下的坍缩Kähler–Ricci流奇点分析提供了新的曲率控制工具。

Abstract: We establish a Type I curvature bound for finite-time volume-collapsing Kähler--Ricci flows on compact Kähler surfaces. No symmetry assumption is imposed, and the initial Kähler class need not be rational. The proof combines local symplectic topology with the classification of gradient Kähler--Ricci shrinking solitons. Bamler's compactness and structure theory provides the shrinking limits used in the argument.

</details>


### [132] [Uniqueness of the soliton vector field of a shrinking gradient Kähler-Ricci soliton](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02164)
*Ronan J. Conlon,Alix Deruelle*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 在双全纯映射拉回的意义下，完备收缩梯度Kähler-Ricci孤子的孤子向量场是唯一的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 收缩梯度Kähler-Ricci孤子的几何结构中，孤子向量场是核心对象，但其是否由度量的存在性唯一确定尚需澄清，本文旨在解决这一唯一性问题。

Method: 利用复几何与Ricci流理论，通过双全纯映射（biholomorphism）的推前作用，刻画孤子向量场的唯一性。

Result: 证明了完备收缩梯度Kähler-Ricci孤子的孤子向量场在相差双全纯映射推前的情形下唯一。

Conclusion: 孤子向量场本质上由孤子度量决定，唯一到双全纯等价。

Abstract: We establish the uniqueness of the soliton vector field of a complete shrinking gradient Kähler-Ricci soliton up to pushforward by biholomorphism.

</details>


### [133] [Ahlfors--Weill Extension, Asymptotically Conformal Curves and Epstein--Poincaré Surfaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02171)
*Ming Hong Tee*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文构造局部拟反射，改进Ahlfors估计，证明渐近共形曲线所围区域的双曲度量与拟双曲度量渐近相似，并证明互补区域的Epstein-Poincaré曲面在曲线附近任意接近。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Ahlfors- Weill构造可将Schwarzian范数小于1/2的单叶函数延拓为Riemann球上的拟共形同胚，该延拓可借助Epstein曲面几何理解；对渐近共形曲线，单值化映射可延拓到更大圆盘，Loewner理论可验证其拟共形性。作者希望在此基础上进一步刻画渐近共形曲线所围区域的度量性质及相关曲面的接近性。

Method: 构造具有该延拓性质的局部拟反射；利用该局部拟反射改进Ahlfors的经典估计；结合Epstein曲面对延拓的几何重释。

Result: 证明渐近共形曲线所围区域具有渐近相似的双曲度量与拟双曲度量；并证明互补区域的Epstein-Poincaré曲面在曲线附近彼此任意接近。

Conclusion: 局部拟反射是研究渐近共形曲线区域度量等价性与Epstein-Poincaré曲面渐近行为的有效工具。

Abstract: The Ahlfors--Weill construction explicitly extends a univalent function $f$ with Schwarzian norm less than $1/2$ to a quasiconformal homeomorphism of the Riemann sphere, an extension that can be understood geometrically through Epstein surfaces. For asymptotically conformal curves, the uniformization map can be extended to a larger disk, and Loewner theory can be used to verify that this extension is quasiconformal. In this paper, we construct a local quasireflection with such an extension. Using this local quasireflection, we modify classical estimates of Ahlfors and show that domains bounded by asymptotically conformal curves have asymptotically similar hyperbolic and quasihyperbolic metrics. Furthermore, by combining these estimates with a geometric reinterpretation of the extension through Epstein surfaces, we show that the Epstein-Poincaré surfaces of the complementary domains are arbitrarily close to one another near the curve.

</details>
